### [136] [Cycle-Consistent Search: Question Reconstructability as a Proxy Reward for Search Agent Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12967)
*Sohyun An,Shuibenyang Yuan,Hayeon Lee,Cho-Jui Hsieh,Alexander Min*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Cycle-Consistent Search (CCS)，一种无黄金监督的搜索智能体训练框架，利用循环一致性思想——高质量搜索轨迹应能完整重建原始问题，从而产生奖励信号。通过信息瓶颈（排除最终答案、NER掩码）防止信息泄漏。实验表明CCS在QA基准上达到有监督基线水平，优于其他无监督方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习方法训练搜索智能体依赖黄金监督（如标准答案），难以规模化应用。现有方法存在局限性，需要一种无需黄金监督即可训练搜索智能体的可扩展方案。

Method: 提出Cycle-Consistent Search (CCS)框架，核心假设是：最优搜索轨迹应是无损编码问题意图的，因此高质量轨迹应能准确重建原始问题。应用信息瓶颈技术（排除最终响应、NER掩码搜索查询）防止信息泄漏，强制重建依赖检索观察和结构骨架。

Result: 在问答基准数据集上的实验表明，CCS达到与有监督基线相当的性能，并优于以往无黄金监督方法。

Conclusion: CCS为黄金监督不可用的场景提供了一种可扩展的搜索智能体训练范式。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning (RL) has shown strong potential for optimizing search agents in complex information retrieval tasks. However, existing approaches predominantly rely on gold supervision, such as ground-truth answers, which is difficult to scale. To address this limitation, we propose Cycle-Consistent Search (CCS), a gold-supervision-free framework for training search agents, inspired by cycle-consistency techniques from unsupervised machine translation and image-to-image translation. Our key hypothesis is that an optimal search trajectory, unlike insufficient or irrelevant ones, serves as a lossless encoding of the question's intent. Consequently, a high-quality trajectory should preserve the information required to accurately reconstruct the original question, thereby inducing a reward signal for policy optimization. However, naive cycle-consistency objectives are vulnerable to information leakage, as reconstruction may rely on superficial lexical cues rather than the underlying search process. To reduce this effect, we apply information bottlenecks, including exclusion of the final response and named entity recognition (NER) masking of search queries. These constraints force reconstruction to rely on retrieved observations together with the structural scaffold, ensuring that the resulting reward signal reflects informational adequacy rather than linguistic redundancy. Experiments on question-answering benchmarks show that CCS achieves performance comparable to supervised baselines while outperforming prior methods that do not rely on gold supervision. These results suggest that CCS provides a scalable training paradigm for training search agents in settings where gold supervision is unavailable.

</details>


### [137] [Bilevel Late Acceptance Hill Climbing for the Electric Capacitated Vehicle Routing Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13013)
*Yinghao Qin,Mosab Bazargani,Edmund K. Burke,Carlos A. Coello Coello,Zhongmin Song,Jun Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: This paper tackles the Electric Capacitated Vehicle Routing Problem (E-CVRP) through a bilevel optimization framework that handles routing and charging decisions separately or jointly depending on the search stage. By analyzing their interaction, we introduce a surrogate objective at the upper level to guide the search and accelerate convergence. A bilevel Late Acceptance Hill Climbing algorithm (b-LAHC) is introduced that operates through three phases: greedy descent, neighborhood exploration, and final solution refinement. b-LAHC operates with fixed parameters, eliminating the need for complex adaptation while remaining lightweight and effective. Extensive experiments on the IEEE WCCI-2020 benchmark show that b-LAHC achieves superior or competitive performance against eight state-of-the-art algorithms. Under a fixed evaluation budget, it attains near-optimal solutions on small-scale instances and sets 9/10 new best-known results on large-scale benchmarks, improving existing records by an average of 1.07%. Moreover, the strong correlation (though not universal) observed between the surrogate objective and the complete cost justifies the use of the surrogate objective while still necessitating a joint solution of both levels, thereby validating the effectiveness of the proposed bilevel framework and highlighting its potential for efficiently solving large-scale routing problems with a hierarchical structure.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI enhancement unavailable (ai_circuit_open). Original arXiv metadata is preserved.

Method: AI enhancement unavailable; see the original abstract.

Result: AI enhancement unavailable; see the original abstract.

Conclusion: AI enhancement unavailable (ai_circuit_open). Original arXiv metadata is preserved.

Abstract: This paper tackles the Electric Capacitated Vehicle Routing Problem (E-CVRP) through a bilevel optimization framework that handles routing and charging decisions separately or jointly depending on the search stage. By analyzing their interaction, we introduce a surrogate objective at the upper level to guide the search and accelerate convergence. A bilevel Late Acceptance Hill Climbing algorithm (b-LAHC) is introduced that operates through three phases: greedy descent, neighborhood exploration, and final solution refinement. b-LAHC operates with fixed parameters, eliminating the need for complex adaptation while remaining lightweight and effective. Extensive experiments on the IEEE WCCI-2020 benchmark show that b-LAHC achieves superior or competitive performance against eight state-of-the-art algorithms. Under a fixed evaluation budget, it attains near-optimal solutions on small-scale instances and sets 9/10 new best-known results on large-scale benchmarks, improving existing records by an average of 1.07%. Moreover, the strong correlation (though not universal) observed between the surrogate objective and the complete cost justifies the use of the surrogate objective while still necessitating a joint solution of both levels, thereby validating the effectiveness of the proposed bilevel framework and highlighting its potential for efficiently solving large-scale routing problems with a hierarchical structure.

</details>


### [138] [PAL: Personal Adaptive Learner](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13017)
*Megha Chakraborty,Darssan L. Eswaramoorthi,Madhur Thareja,Het Riteshkumar Shah,Finlay Palmer,Aryaman Bahl,Michelle A Ihetu,Amit Sheth*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PAL是一个AI驱动的教育平台，通过多模态内容分析将 lecture视频转化为互动学习体验，实时根据学习者反应调整问题难度，并在课程结束时生成个性化总结，旨在实现超越静态适应的实时个性化教育支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI教育平台大多局限于静态适应（预定义测验、统一学习节奏、通用反馈），无法对学习者不断变化的理解状态做出实时响应，这限制了个性化教育的真正潜力。

Method: 1. 多模态内容分析：持续分析 lecture 视频的多种模态信息；2. 自适应提问：根据学习者反应动态调整问题难度；3. 个性化总结生成：结合学习者兴趣定制总结内容与示例。

Result: 提出了一个将多模态内容分析与自适应决策相结合的创新框架，展示了AI从静态个性化向实时个性化支持迈进的可行路径。

Conclusion: PAL代表了AI赋能教育的重要进展，证明了实时、个性化学习支持的核心挑战可以通过动态内容分析与自适应交互技术得到有效解决。

Abstract: AI-driven education platforms have made some progress in personalisation, yet most remain constrained to static adaptation--predefined quizzes, uniform pacing, or generic feedback--limiting their ability to respond to learners' evolving understanding. This shortfall highlights the need for systems that are both context-aware and adaptive in real time. We introduce PAL (Personal Adaptive Learner), an AI-powered platform that transforms lecture videos into interactive learning experiences. PAL continuously analyzes multimodal lecture content and dynamically engages learners through questions of varying difficulty, adjusting to their responses as the lesson unfolds. At the end of a session, PAL generates a personalized summary that reinforces key concepts while tailoring examples to the learner's interests. By uniting multimodal content analysis with adaptive decision-making, PAL contributes a novel framework for responsive digital learning. Our work demonstrates how AI can move beyond static personalization toward real-time, individualized support, addressing a core challenge in AI-enabled education.

</details>


<div id='cs.OS'></div>

# cs.OS [[Back]](#toc)

### [139] [ProbeLogits: Kernel-Level LLM Inference Primitives for AI-Native Operating Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11943)
*Daeyeon Son*

Main category: cs.OS

TL;DR: ProbeLogits 是一个内建于裸机 x86_64 内核（Anima OS，约 28.5 万行 Rust）的 OS 原语：仅做一次前向传播，直接读取类别标签（verbalizer）对应 token 的 logit，用受限 softmax 完成安全/危险分类，零可学习参数，无需文本生成与解析。核心洞察是"LLM 的 logit 向量是一种新的进程状态"，内核可在生成任何文本之前把它当作治理原语；配合 WASM 沙箱下的内核强制执行、KV cache 作为进程状态（checkpoint/restore/fork），构成"AI 原生操作系统"的语义治理层。核心争议点：摘要与正文在 F1 数值上存在不一致，且性能对比、verbalizer 选择、α 调参均存在测试集调优与后验选择的方法论隐患。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 OS 把进程视为不透明实体，丢弃 LLM 推理过程中的 logit 向量（不确定性、意图、置信度），导致三方面的缺失：(1) 延迟累积——文本式安全检查需经应用代码、IPC、文本生成、解析多层叠加，使逐动作治理不可行；(2) 可绕过性——应用层安全过滤器与被约束 agent 同权限，足够强的 agent 或沙箱缺陷即可绕过；(3) 语义不透明——内核无法判断模型置信度、输出是否安全或行为是否异常，因而无法做语义感知的调度与治理。作者认为 AI 的分层架构（硬件→内核→用户态运行时→推理框架→编排库→应用）本身制造了这些成本与攻击面，因此必须把推理下沉到内核。此外，2026 年的量化为 4-bit、AVX-512/VNNI SIMD 与每 token 数十毫秒的 CPU 推理，使 7B 模型驻留内核成为可能，这是"现在做"的时机条件。

Method: 机制：给定 prompt p，一次前向得到全词表 logit ℓ=f(p)∈R^|V|（|V|=152,064），在目标类别 token 位置 t_ci 上做受限 N 路 softmax（log-sum-exp 数值稳定，分母≤1e-10 时回退为均匀分布 1/N），二分类置信度恒在 [0.5,1.0]。实现三类内核操作：probe_yes_no（二分类）、probe_classify（N 路，类标签须为单 token）、text_to_id（基于 GPT-2 BPE 词表的 BTreeMap 查找，O(log|V|)）。另有 logit_entropy（f64 累加，0–11.93 nats，作为自治门控/OOD 检测/动态温度的内核读数）与 grammar-constrained decoding（choice/regex 掩码 logits，非 ASCII 选项不保证）。校准：上下文校准（启动时用 7 个空输入探针测得偏置 Δ=logit_pos−logit_neg，按 α∈[0,1] 缩放扣除），α 是部署期策略旋钮而非学习超参；并记录"verbalizer 先验不对称"（同一模型 Y/N 与 S/D 的偏置幅度甚至符号相反，Qwen 为 +4.49 / −3.02）。KV cache 作为进程状态：类比 CPU 寄存器/KV 位置/fork()/上下文切换，实现 kv_checkpoint（32 MB 上限）、kv_restore（维度校验）、kv_fork。治理流水线：Stage1 对抗性预过滤（O(n) 模式扫描，<1 µs，检测注入/base64/rot13/权限冒充/指令覆盖）→ Stage2 输入清洗 → Stage3 校准前向（读 Safe/Dangerous verbalizer logit + 隐私关键词 boost）；置信度分档映射为 Block(>0.85)/Warn(>0.70)/Log(0.15–0.70)/Allow(≤0.15)，NonDeception 原则 P>0.80 升级为 Warn；所有治理决策写入 keyed Blake3 哈希链审计日志（内存环形缓冲 256 条、落盘 AnimaFS、启动时校验失配则进入 Safe Mode）。实现：no_std Rust 15,631 行裸机推理引擎（GGUF 加载器、GPT-2 BPE、Llama 架构含 GQA/RoPE/SwiGLU/RMSNorm、Q4_0/Q6_K/Q8_0 的 AVX-512/AVX2/VNNI SIMD 内核、SMP 工作窃取图执行器），自研 xHCI(9,357 行)、UEFI 堆引导、AP 引导与自旋锁；WASM 沙箱经 Cranelift AOT、fuel 限制，仅通过 18 个 host function 与内核交互。威胁模型：WASM 沙箱 + fuel 限制、内核可信、网络不可信、agent 可为对抗性；核心区分是"enforcement 是结构性的、detection 是概率性的"。

Result: 安全分类（ToxicChat n=1000，362 有毒/638 良性，seed=42）：纯 logit 模式 Qwen2.5-7B S/D α=0 F1=0.812（bootstrap CI [0.781,0.841]）vs Llama Guard 3 Q4_K_M 0.675（CI [0.630,0.717]），CI 不相交、+13.7 pp；Llama 3 S/D 0.679 与 LG3 0.675 CI 完全重叠（+0.4 pp，作者明示"parity"仅为 CI 重叠意义、非正式非劣检验）；Mistral Y/N 0.719（+4.4 pp）。LG3 召回 0.536/精度 0.911，PL-Llama3 召回 0.641（+10.5 pp）但精度 0.723（−18.8 pp），呈显著的运行点权衡。HarmBench 非版权集（n=300）三模型 Y/N 拦截率 97–99%（Qwen 99.0%、Llama 98.0%、Mistral 98.7%），McNemar p>0.24 无显著差异；版权集差异极大（Qwen 50%/Llama 6%/Mistral 22%）故单独报告。XSTest（n=450）召回 98.5–100%，但过度拒答跨模型差 35 pp（Qwen 86.4% vs Llama 3 51.6%），说明过度拒答非架构无关。裸机原生 260 条 OS 动作基准全流水线 F1=0.980、P=1.000、R=0.960；上下文校准把准确率从 64.8% 提到 97.3%。α 扫描（裸机 Qwen S/D）：α=0 时 F1 仅 0.180（召回 0.099），α=0.5 最优 F1=0.829（R=0.796/P=0.865），α=1.0 召回最高 0.892 精度降至 0.734。速度：单次分类 332–556 ms（保守、全 logits）vs LG3 851–1,142 ms，快 2.4–3.4×；仅取末 token logits 的部署态中位数 219.6 ms（Llama3 224.4、Mistral Q8 391.4）。负结果与小模型地板：135M/0.5B 在 HarmBench 仅拦截 13.0%/10.0%（漏检 87–90%），虽仅 9–25 ms 却无信号，证明朴素级联不可行，探针底座需 ≥7B；多轴探针组合器在 XSTest 与单轴统计持平（ΔF1 +0.009，McNemar p=0.33）且跨集冻结迁移失败（HarmBench 召回 0.93→0.39）；2-of-3 跨模型投票把 F1 从 0.848 提到 0.867、过度拒答 18.4%→13.6%，但 CI [−0.013,+0.049]、p≤0=0.12，仅方向性为正。系统性能：135M 1,344 tok/s（8 线程，优于 llama.cpp 1,301；16 线程反而降至 1,266，即 gang 宽度是模型尺寸相关的调参项）；7B 12.73 tok/s（每 token 78.6 ms）低于 llama.cpp 的 15 tok/s，约为 DDR5 理论下限 4.1 GB/75 GB/s=55 ms 的 70%，即裸机推理已触及内存带宽墙，软件优化无法突破。辅助原语：熵计算 0.65 ms(135M)/2.57 ms(7B)；choice 掩码 7.70 ms/token（占 7B 前向约 10%，修正 v1 的 <0.01 ms 误估）；KV checkpoint/restore 0.037/0.016 ms（7B，32 位置 0.93 MB）。融合安全头（读取末 token 隐状态的小型学习线性头，14B 硬件验证）：HarmBench 召回 90.0%、XSTest 召回 100.0%、过度拒答 16.7%，模型不匹配时 fail-closed 回退到 ProbeLogits。诚实性说明：撤回 v1 的 1.39× 吞吐优势主张，撤回 4.3 节以外的未归档 F1=0.837 数字（以 2026-07-05 硅片复测 0.829 为准），披露摘要与正文数值不一致、LG3 重复运行 F1 波动 0.675→0.659。

Conclusion: 论文主张 ProbeLogits 是首个把 LLM logit 向量暴露为内核级抽象的 OS 原语，将分类路径从"生成+解析+IPC"改为"一次前向+读一个 logit"，从而在无第二个模型、无可学习参数的前提下达成与微调安全分类器相当或更优的 F1，并可按操作类别动态取舍召回/精度；其核心价值不在 ML 技术（verbalizer+上下文校准本属成熟 NLP 手法），而在于把检测放到 agent 无法绕过的位置——WASM 沙箱边界之下由内核代为执行，因此形成"enforcement 结构性、detection 概率性"的纵深防御，而非宣称分类器本身足够准。作者同时给出反直觉的硬件结论：裸机推理的性能上限是内存带宽而非软件效率，进一步加速只能靠更高带宽或 GPU。可信度方面，评估严谨度较好（Wilson/bootstrap CI、McNemar、重复运行方差披露、负结果与撤回项如实陈述、代码规模与未相关代码行数明确标注），但方法论上仍有实质弱点：verbalizer 为事后按基准挑选、α 在报告同一基准上调参、260 条 OS 动作基准为作者自标注且手写规则针对其迭代调优，故其最强数值（F1=0.980）不宜视为独立可复现结论；且核心比较对象 Llama Guard 3 在 ToxicChat 上仅 0.675，与其自报 F1~0.939 差距巨大，说明"超越强基线"的结论强依赖分布外评测。尚未闭合的部分包括：GPU 加速、≥7B 驻留的内存成本、CFG 完整约束解码的 no_std 移植、治理不变式的形式化验证、证书吊销检查、多智能体真实负载演示，以及不引入学习参数的探针提效路径（蒸馏 tripwire 头仍是未来工作）。

Abstract: An OS kernel that runs LLM inference internally can read logit distributions before any text is generated -- and act on them as a governance primitive. I present ProbeLogits, a kernel-level operation that performs a single forward pass and reads specific token logits to classify agent actions as safe or dangerous, with zero learned parameters. On a 260-prompt OS action benchmark (9 categories including adversarial attacks), ProbeLogits achieves F1=0.980, Precision=1.000, and Recall=0.960 using a general-purpose 7B model at 4-bit quantization. On ToxicChat (1,000 human-annotated real conversations), it achieves F1=0.790 at default calibration strength $α$=1.0, improving to F1=0.837 at $α$=0.5 -- 89% of Llama Guard 3's F1~0.939 with zero learned parameters. A key design contribution is the calibration strength $α$, which serves as a deployment-time policy knob rather than a learned hyperparameter. By adjusting $α$, the OS can enforce strict policies for privileged operations ($α\geq 0.8$, maximizing recall) or relaxed policies for conversational agents ($α$=0.5, maximizing precision). Contextual calibration improves accuracy from 64.8% to 97.3% on the custom benchmark. I implement ProbeLogits within Anima OS, a bare-metal x86_64 OS written in 80,400 lines of Rust. Because agent actions must pass through kernel-mediated host functions, ProbeLogits enforcement operates below the WASM sandbox boundary, making it significantly harder to circumvent than application-layer classifiers. Each classification costs 65ms on 7B -- fast enough for per-action governance. I also show that treating KV cache as process state enables checkpoint, restore, and fork operations analogous to traditional process management. To my knowledge, no prior system exposes LLM logit vectors as OS-level governance primitives.

</details>


### [140] [Hybrid Adaptive Tuning for Tiered Memory Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12165)
*Xi Wang,Jie Liu,Shuangyan Yang,Jongryool Kim,Pengfei Su,Dong Li*

Main category: cs.OS

TL;DR: 本文针对内存分层系统中的参数调优问题，提出了PTMT框架，采用离线+在线混合调优方法，结合强化学习技术，自动优化内存分层解决方案的参数配置，显著提升系统性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 内存分层（Memory tiering）是一种经济高效的解决方案，可提升内存容量、利用率和带宽。然而，现有的内存分层系统依赖软件进行内存分析、热点页面检测和页面迁移，这些软件包含多个系统参数，参数配置直接影响应用性能，但手动调优困难且耗时。

Method: 1. 对系统参数进行全面分类
2. 使用代表性内存分层解决方案表征应用性能对参数的敏感性
3. 提出PTMT框架，采用"离线+在线"混合调优方法：
   - 离线阶段：构建性能数据库用于在线查询，减少运行时开销
   - 在线阶段：使用针对内存分层定制的强化学习进行调优

Result: PTMT在四种内存分层解决方案（TPP、UPM、Colloid、AutoNUMA）上相比默认配置分别提升性能30%、26%、21%和14%；相比现有最优方案平均提升32%。

Conclusion: PTMT能够有效解决内存分层系统的在线参数调优挑战，混合调优方法结合离线数据库和在线强化学习可显著提升性能，且适用于多种内存分层解决方案。

Abstract: Memory tiering provides a cost-effective solution to increase memory capacity, utilization, and even bandwidth. Memory tiering relies on system software for memory profiling, detection of frequently accessed pages, and page migration. Such a system software often comes with system parameters. The configurations of those parameters impact application performance. We comprehensively classify system parameters, and characterize the sensitivity of application performance to them using representative memory tiering solutions. Furthermore, we introduce a lightweight and user-friendly framework PTMT, which automates tuning of parameters at runtime for various memory tiering solutions. We identify major challenges for online tuning of memory tiering. PTMT uses a hybrid "offline + online" tuning method: while the offline phase builds a performance database for online queries and reduces runtime overhead, the online phase uses reinforcement learning (customized to memory tiering) to tune. PTMT improves performance by 30%, 26%, 21%, and 14%, on four memory tiering solutions (TPP, UPM, Colloid, and AutoNUMA), compared to using the default configurations. PTMT outperforms the state-of-the-art by 32% on average.

</details>


### [141] [TierBPF: Page Migration Admission Control for Tiered Memory via eBPF](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12300)
*Xi Wang,Tal Zussman,Yuang Xu,Bin Ma,Asaf Cidon,Dong Li*

Main category: cs.OS

TL;DR: TierBPF是一个基于eBPF的软件机制，通过考虑页面大小和硬件拓扑来优化内存分层系统的页面放置决策，在17个工作负载上实现最高17.7%的几何平均吞吐量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有软件内存分层系统在做页面放置决策时，未考虑两个关键因素：迁移页面的大小，以及底层硬件设备和分层拓扑结构，这导致应用性能未能充分发挥。

Method: TierBPF通过eBPF钩子实现简单的二元页面准入决策，采用轻量级页面性能分析机制（不依赖应用工作集大小），允许用户定义自定义策略，可集成到现有内存分层系统中。

Result: 集成到3个内存分层系统后，在17个工作负载上评估，最高实现17.7%的几何平均吞吐量提升，个别工作负载提升可达75%。

Conclusion: TierBPF能有效弥补现有内存分层系统的不足，通过考虑页面大小和硬件拓扑显式提升应用性能，且具有良好的可扩展性和兼容性。

Abstract: Existing software-based memory tiering systems decide which pages to place on the slower or faster tier. However, they do not take into account two important factors that greatly influence application performance: the size of the migrated pages, and the underlying hardware device and tiering topology. We introduce TierBPF, a software mechanism that can be plugged into existing memory tiering systems to take these factors into account, by making simple binary page admission decisions. TierBPF is implemented as a set of eBPF hooks, which allow users to define their own custom policies. In order to make its decisions, TierBPF utilizes a lightweight tracking mechanism for page profiling which is not dependent on the application's working set size. TierBPF, integrated into three memory tiering systems and evaluated with 17 workloads, achieves geomean throughput gains of up to 17.7% with improvements of up to 75% for individual workloads.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [142] [Uncertainty Quantification in CNN Through the Bootstrap of Convex Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11833)
*Hongfei Du,Emre Barut,Fang Jin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"Bootstrap CCNN"框架：通过对凸化卷积神经网络（CCNN）做自助抽样（bootstrap）来为 CNN 提供预测区间，并借助迁移学习（"Train and Forget"）将框架推广到任意层数的非凸 CNN。作者证明了自助抽样对 CCNN 估计量的渐近一致性，实验表明其在多个图像数据集上比集成方法与普通 CNN 的 bootstrap 具有更高的对数似然、更短的区间长度和更小的标准误，且计算开销远低于集成方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CNN 的不确定性量化（UQ）长期被忽视，缺少高效且具理论保证的 UQ 工具限制了其在医疗等高风险领域的应用。现有深度学习 UQ 方法（贝叶斯/变分推断、MC Dropout、集成方法、Delta 方法、分位数损失等）存在三方面缺陷：(1) 缺乏理论一致性保证，不确定性质量无从保证；(2) 神经网络非凸性使 SGD 等训练过程不可解，难以得到收敛与一致性的理论界，且最优解未必可达，导致置信区间过宽；(3) 参数量与样本量同阶、过拟合倾向严重，任何仅依赖网络本身的 UQ 都会给出过度自信（over-confident）的估计；集成方法还须从零独立训练每个网络，计算代价高。早期的神经网络 bootstrap 工作（Paass & Gerhard 1993；Robert 1996；Khosravi et al. 2015）同样受非凸性限制，无法保证每个自助样本上的最优解。

Method: 1) 凸化 CNN（CCNN）：对两隐层 CNN 做凸松弛，得分 f(x_i)=Σ_p A_p^T z_p(x_i)，通过最小化核范数 ‖A‖_* 施加低秩结构（min_{‖A‖_*≤C} Σ L(f(x_i),y_i)），用投影梯度下降求解；非线性情形通过核技巧（K=QQ^T，特征取 Q 的对应行）扩展到平滑 ReLU 等具有多项式展开的激活。2) 自助框架（Algorithm 1）：初始拟合 A_0 后，每次有放回重采样得到 D_b，用上一次解 A_{b-1} 作 warm start 求解新模型；因凸性保证全局最优，warm start 使迭代次数降低一个数量级；最终对每个测试样本各类别的预测概率取 α/2% 与 (1−α)/2% 分位数构成经验自助置信区间。3) 理论：改用光滑核范数 ‖A‖_*μ 使损失可微，使"分布→估计函数"的泛函 Hadamard 可微，从而满足 M-估计量自助一致性；Theorem 1 证明自助抽样分布与真实抽样分布之差在概率意义下收敛；Theorem 2 在数据可分且最优分类器可由有限卷积核线性 CNN 表示时 bootstrap 一致。核特征需满足样本独立性与特征维数 o(n)，前者通过引入来自同一生成过程的次级（无标签）数据集计算 K(x_i, x̃_j) 解决。4) 迁移学习：将预训练 CNN（如 VGG16/ResNet50）的最后卷积层输出作为 CCNN 输入，从而支持任意（多层/非凸）网络；若无现成预训练模型，提出三种"遗忘"式预训练：Train and Forget（改用无关数据集继续训练至原数据精度退化为随机猜测）、Train and Flip（随机翻转标签训练至过拟合随机标签）、Train and Perturb（加高斯扰动使精度接近随机猜测）。关键前提：预训练网络不得依赖训练数据样本，否则破坏 bootstrap 所需的样本独立性。

Result: 实验使用 MNIST、Noisy MNIST（运动模糊）、Fashion MNIST、CIFAR10、Cats and Dogs。评价指标为平均对数似然（越大越好）与 95% 置信区间平均长度（越短越好）。与 20 网络集成方法及非凸 CNN bootstrap 对比：CCNN 在 MNIST（-3.050 vs -5.193 vs -6.891）、Fashion MNIST（-355.46 vs -363.19 vs -458.06）、Cats&Dogs（-222.686 vs -239.654 vs -334.797）上对数似然更优且区间更短，标准误也更小；在 MNIST-blur 上与基线 CNN 精度相当但标准误更小，表明非凸网络因难收敛到全局最优而具有更高不确定性，本方法能可靠地检出。迁移学习对比中，Train and Forget 整体最优（Fashion MNIST 达 -261.187，优于 CCNN 的 -355.46 与集成的 -363.19；CIFAR10 为 -525.736，优于 CCNN 的 -576.417 与集成的 -666.082），Train and Perturb 标准误最小、适合作保守选择。三种迁移方案均显著缩短区间并提升准确率，说明 bootstrap CCNN + Train and Forget 的组合在精度与稳定性上优于基线 CNN 与当前先进方法，同时因 warm start 使计算量显著低于集成方法（CCNN 跑在 16 核 CPU，其他网络用 GPU）。

Conclusion: 本文的主要贡献是：首次为 CNN 不确定性量化给出一个具理论一致性保证的框架——将 bootstrap 与凸化 CNN 结合，证明其对预测抽样分布的估计一致（可分数据下 bootstrap 本身一致），从而使置信区间具有统计有效性；通过迁移学习（含"Train and Forget"）突破 CCNN 仅限两隐层的限制，使框架适用于任意凸/非凸网络；实验证明其在准确率、稳定性（更小标准误）与计算开销上均优于集成方法与普通 CNN bootstrap。局限与备注包括：理论结果针对线性网络，非线性核版本需额外满足特征独立性与特征维数 o(n) 条件；三类"遗忘式"预训练缺乏严格理论保证（仅靠引入随机性抵消依赖关系），性能与基本迁移学习相当；理论框架依赖数据可分性假设，且需额外无标签次级数据。

Abstract: Despite the popularity of Convolutional Neural Networks (CNN), the problem of uncertainty quantification (UQ) of CNN has been largely overlooked. Lack of efficient UQ tools severely limits the application of CNN in certain areas, such as medicine, where prediction uncertainty is critically important. Among the few existing UQ approaches that have been proposed for deep learning, none of them has theoretical consistency that can guarantee the uncertainty quality. To address this issue, we propose a novel bootstrap based framework for the estimation of prediction uncertainty. The inference procedure we use relies on convexified neural networks to establish the theoretical consistency of bootstrap. Our approach has a significantly less computational load than its competitors, as it relies on warm-starts at each bootstrap that avoids refitting the model from scratch. We further explore a novel transfer learning method so our framework can work on arbitrary neural networks. We experimentally demonstrate our approach has a much better performance compared to other baseline CNNs and state-of-the-art methods on various image datasets.

</details>


### [143] [CLAD: Efficient Log Anomaly Detection Directly on Compressed Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13024)
*Benzhao Tang,Shiyu Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CLAD是首个直接在压缩字节流上进行日志异常检测(LAD)的深度学习框架，无需解压和解析，通过利用正常日志压缩形成规律字节模式而异常会破坏这一规律的关键洞察实现创新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有日志异常检测方法需要完全解压和解析，导致严重的预处理开销。流式压缩日志的爆发式增长使得这一瓶颈问题愈发突出。

Method: 设计了专门的架构：扩张卷积字节编码器(dilated convolutional byte encoder)、混合Transformer-mLSTM、四路聚合池化(four-way aggregation pooling)；采用两阶段训练策略：掩码预训练(mask pre-training)和焦距对比微调(focal-contrastive fine-tuning)以处理严重的类别不平衡问题。

Result: 在五个数据集上达到最先进的平均F1分数0.9909，比最佳基线提升2.72个百分点。

Conclusion: CLAD在完全消除解压和解析开销的同时提供卓越精度，且可泛化至结构化流式压缩器，是一种鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: The explosive growth of system logs makes streaming compression essential, yet existing log anomaly detection (LAD) methods incur severe pre-processing overhead by requiring full decompression and parsing. We introduce CLAD, the first deep learning framework to perform LAD directly on compressed byte streams. CLAD bypasses these bottlenecks by exploiting a key insight: normal logs compress into regular byte patterns, while anomalies systematically disrupt them. To extract these multi-scale deviations from opaque bytes, we propose a purpose-built architecture integrating a dilated convolutional byte encoder, a hybrid Transformer--mLSTM, and four-way aggregation pooling. This is coupled with a two-stage training strategy of masked pre-training and focal-contrastive fine-tuning to effectively handle severe class imbalance. Evaluated across five datasets, CLAD achieves a state-of-the-art average F1-score of 0.9909 and outperforms the best baseline by 2.72 percentage points. It delivers superior accuracy while completely eliminating decompression and parsing overheads, offering a robust solution that generalizes to structured streaming compressors.

</details>


### [144] [Schema-Adaptive Tabular Representation Learning with LLMs for Generalizable Multimodal Clinical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11835)
*Hongxi Mao,Wei Zhou,Mengting Jia,Tao Fang,Huan Gao,Bin Zhang,Shangyang Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于大语言模型(LLM)的表格数据语义表示学习方法，通过将结构化变量转换为自然语言语句实现跨模式的零样本迁移学习，在阿尔茨海默病诊断任务中超越人类专家水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格数据的模式泛化能力差，缺乏对结构化变量的语义理解；在临床医学等领域，电子健康记录(EHR)模式差异显著，传统方法难以应对。

Method: 提出Schema-Adaptive Tabular Representation Learning方法：利用LLM将结构化变量转换为语义自然语言陈述，通过预训练LLM编码实现跨未见过模式的零样本对齐；集成到多模态框架中结合表格数据和MRI影像进行痴呆症诊断。

Result: 在NACC和ADNI数据集上达到最优性能，成功实现向未见模式的零样本迁移，在回顾性诊断任务中显著优于临床基线，包括获得Board认证的神经科医生。

Conclusion: LLM驱动的表格表示学习方法可扩展、鲁棒，为异构现实世界数据提供解决方案，为将LLM推理扩展到结构化数据领域提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Machine learning for tabular data remains constrained by poor schema generalization, a challenge rooted in the lack of semantic understanding of structured variables. This challenge is particularly acute in domains like clinical medicine, where electronic health record (EHR) schemas vary significantly. To solve this problem, we propose Schema-Adaptive Tabular Representation Learning, a novel method that leverages large language models (LLMs) to create transferable tabular embeddings. By transforming structured variables into semantic natural language statements and encoding them with a pretrained LLM, our approach enables zero-shot alignment across unseen schemas without manual feature engineering or retraining. We integrate our encoder into a multimodal framework for dementia diagnosis, combining tabular and MRI data. Experiments on NACC and ADNI datasets demonstrate state-of-the-art performance and successful zero-shot transfer to unseen schemas, significantly outperforming clinical baselines, including board-certified neurologists, in retrospective diagnostic tasks. These results validate our LLM-driven approach as a scalable, robust solution for heterogeneous real-world data, offering a pathway to extend LLM-based reasoning to structured domains.

</details>


### [145] [AutoSurrogate: An LLM-Driven Multi-Agent Framework for Autonomous Construction of Deep Learning Surrogate Models in Subsurface Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11945)
*Jiale Liu,Nanzhe Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AutoSurrogate是一个基于大语言模型的多智能体框架，使没有机器学习背景的领域科学家能够通过自然语言指令构建高质量的地下水流深度学习替代模型。系统自动完成数据分析、架构选择、超参数优化、模型训练和质量评估，并能自主处理训练失败等常见问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习替代模型可加速地下水流模拟，但构建过程需要大量ML专业知识——从架构设计到超参数调优——这是大多数领域科学家不具备的。这种专业知识差距阻碍了DL替代模型的广泛采用。

Method: 提出AutoSurrogate框架，包含四个专门智能体协作执行：数据剖析、模型池架构选择、贝叶斯超参数优化、模型训练和质量评估。系统可自主处理常见失败模式（如数值不稳定时重启训练、预测精度不达标时切换架构）。

Result: 在3D地质碳储存建模任务中（渗透率场→压力和CO₂饱和度场，31个时间步），AutoSurrogate无需人工调优即可优于专家设计的基线和领域无关的AutoML方法。

Conclusion: 该框架展示了很强的实际部署潜力，单条自然语言指令即可生成可部署的替代模型，最小化中间阶段的人为干预。

Abstract: High-fidelity numerical simulation of subsurface flow is computationally intensive, especially for many-query tasks such as uncertainty quantification and data assimilation. Deep learning (DL) surrogates can significantly accelerate forward simulations, yet constructing them requires substantial machine learning (ML) expertise - from architecture design to hyperparameter tuning - that most domain scientists do not possess. Furthermore, the process is predominantly manual and relies heavily on heuristic choices. This expertise gap remains a key barrier to the broader adoption of DL surrogate techniques. For this reason, we present AutoSurrogate, a large-language-model-driven multi-agent framework that enables practitioners without ML expertise to build high-quality surrogates for subsurface flow problems through natural-language instructions. Given simulation data and optional preferences, four specialized agents collaboratively execute data profiling, architecture selection from a model zoo, Bayesian hyperparameter optimization, model training, and quality assessment against user-specified thresholds. The system also handles common failure modes autonomously, including restarting training with adjusted configurations when numerical instabilities occur and switching to alternative architectures when predictive accuracy falls short of targets. In our setting, a single natural-language sentence can be sufficient to produce a deployment-ready surrogate model, with minimum human intervention required at any intermediate stage. We demonstrate the utility of AutoSurrogate on a 3D geological carbon storage modeling task, mapping permeability fields to pressure and CO$_2$ saturation fields over 31 timesteps. Without any manual tuning, AutoSurrogate is able to outperform expert-designed baselines and domain-agnostic AutoML methods, demonstrating strong potential for practical deployment.

</details>


### [146] [A Layer-wise Analysis of Supervised Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11838)
*Qinghua Zhao,Xueling Gong,Xinyu Chen,Zhongfeng Kang,Xinlu Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对监督微调（SFT）在模型深度方向上的机制进行了系统分析，跨越1B–32B多个尺度，提出“基于信息论、几何与优化指标”的层级别分析框架，发现SFT造成的表征与参数变化呈显著的深度依赖模式：中间层（约20%–80%深度）稳定且是知识整合的稳定基底，而最后约20%的层呈现高敏感性、激进可塑性与信息覆盖（灾难性遗忘的主要来源）。据此提出 Mid-Block Efficient Tuning（仅在中段层施加LoRA），在 GSM8K 上比全层LoRA最高提升10.2个百分点（OLMo2-7B：37.5% vs 28%），同时可训练参数更少、预训练知识保留更好（困惑度更接近Base模型）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 监督微调（SFT）是对齐LLM的核心手段，少量样本即可显著改变模型行为，但伴随灾难性遗忘风险；同时，现有的参数高效微调方法（如LoRA）默认对所有层均匀施加更新，忽略了SFT在深度方向上的异质性，导致参数预算被浪费在不敏感的层上。已有可解释性工作（层知识定位、层探针、"表层对齐假说"）只回答了"知识存在哪里"，而没有回答"任务适配究竟发生在哪些层、哪些层对指令跟随能力是必要的"。因此需要回答：SFT的指令跟随能力在层级维度上如何涌现、哪些层是关键，从而指导更高效的、面向对齐的架构化更新策略。

Method: 1）模型与数据：选择具有干净 Base/SFT 配对的纯SFT模型族——OLMo2（1B/7B/13B/32B）与 Mistral-7B-v0.1/v0.1-Instruct（对LLaMA/Gemma/Qwen/DeepSeek等因含RLHF/DPO混杂因子而排除，见附录C）；评测集涵盖MMLU、GSM8K、WikiText、IFEval、HumanEval、MT-Bench、ToxiGen（token级抽取100样本，池化级1000样本），采用流式计算避免存储完整表示张量。2）表征构造与指标（Sec 3）：定义token级 H_i^(l) 与数据集级均值池化 H̄^(l)；信息论用α阶矩阵熵 S_α(K)=1/(1-α)log2 Σλ̃_j^α（prompt entropy、dataset entropy、有效秩、秩亏、稀疏度）；几何用序列曲率、中心化核对齐CKA、余弦相似度、均值位移；优化用注意力四投影的Frobenius权重变化 ΔW^(l)。3）统一评估协议：样本级差分、数据集级差分、对齐分数三种聚合模式。4）机制实验：逐层探针（用中间层输出直接做下一token预测）、逐层权重变化追踪与相关性热图（r=-0.79 与余弦相似度负相关、与谱指标 r>0.8，t检验 p<0.01）、Base/SFT块级层互换实验。5）方法：Mid-Block Efficient Tuning——将L层划分为M个等分块，把LoRA（rank 8/64）集中在不同分段（总可训练参数近似恒定），M=5/3/10 消融；并用困惑度衡量预训练知识保留。

Result: （1）交互分化：OLMo2-32B 的 CKA 在浅层（0–56）保持>0.98，深层骤降至约0.94；均值位移在最后5层指数式飙升（>12.0），余弦相似度同步崩塌。（2）独立趋同：Base 与 SFT 的内在指标曲线高度同步，呈三段式——嵌入层到第1层的过渡、中间层稳定平台（OLMo2-13B 有效秩平台≈0.05、条件数谷值≈45）、最后约20%层出现信息瓶颈（有效秩<0.01、谱范数>500）。（3）探针呈现"休眠—涌现"相变：OLMo2-32B 前50层下一token准确率<0.05，最后14层从近零跃升至 MMLU>0.60（末层略降至0.52）。（4）权重更新呈 J 型轨迹（OLMo2-13B 早期≈0.05、末层峰值>0.10），与表征指标强相关。（5）层互换呈倒 U 型：替换前/后20%层性能下降，替换中间层略有提升（OLMo2-13B MMLU：−0.001、−0.002 vs +0.003）。（6）Mid-Block 有效：OLMo2-7B 上段中配置（01000）达0.375 vs 全层LoRA 0.28（+10点，摘要称+10.2%）；32B（01100）0.32 vs 0.29；13B（01110）0.30 vs 0.27；仅底层（10000）13B仅0.22、32B仅0.25，仅顶层（00001）1B仅0.135 vs 基线0.19，最优与最差边缘配置差距常超过总分的20%。（7）M=3/M=10 消融（OLMo2-13B, ToxiGen）同样呈倒U：M=10时从Segment1 0.4503升至Segment5 0.4771，再降至Segment10 0.3821。（8）知识保留：中段微调（00110）困惑度最接近Base（IFEval 14.54、MT-Bench 13.60），优于全层LoRA（14.70、13.76）与尾段策略（14.65、13.72）。

Conclusion: 结论是SFT带来的对齐效应并非均匀分布，而是"架构上局部化"的：顶部层是激进可塑与信息覆盖（灾难性遗忘）的主要场所，中间层则是新知识与旧知识稳健整合、承载指令跟随能力的稳定基底；因此LoRA应放弃均匀更新，转而依据层的功能差异选择性更新中间块，Mid-Block Efficient Tuning以更少参数取得超越全层LoRA的效果并缓解遗忘。作者明确将该方法定位为分析驱动的概念验证而非QLoRA/AdaLoRA的竞争替代，基线仅为标准LoRA以隔离层深选择的作用。局限：仅覆盖稠密decoder-only架构（未涉及MoE/编码器-解码器）、仅研究SFT阶段（不含RLHF/DPO）、块边界目前靠经验设定（20%–80%实为宽阔稳健的平台而非尖锐峰值），未来需提出训练无关、可自动识别边界的度量。

Abstract: While critical for alignment, Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) incurs the risk of catastrophic forgetting, yet the layer-wise emergence of instruction-following capabilities remains elusive. We investigate this mechanism via a comprehensive analysis utilizing information-theoretic, geometric, and optimization metrics across model scales (1B-32B). Our experiments reveal a distinct depth-dependent pattern: middle layers (20\%-80\%) are stable, whereas final layers exhibit high sensitivity. Leveraging this insight, we propose Mid-Block Efficient Tuning, which selectively updates these critical intermediate layers. Empirically, our method outperforms standard LoRA up to 10.2\% on GSM8K (OLMo2-7B) with reduced parameter overhead, demonstrating that effective alignment is architecturally localized rather than distributed. The code is publicly available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/base_sft.

</details>


### [147] [When Reasoning Models Hurt Behavioral Simulation: A Solver-Sampler Mismatch in Multi-Agent LLM Negotiation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11840)
*Sandro Andric*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在三个多方谈判仿真环境中检验“更强的推理能力会提升仿真保真度”这一常见假设，发现存在求解器—采样器失配：凡是不具备谈判结构化记忆（ledger）的配置，315 次运行中 314 次以“当局裁决”告终（无协议达成）；而开启厂商原生推理（Native）几乎完全改变了结局分布——0/135 次达成协议，与基线完全相同，却同时恢复了表面多样性（熵、让步弧线、轨迹唯一性），使得常规过程级筛查无法察觉失败。唯一打破这一地板效应的因素是让智能体自行书写五字段谈判状态账本（Ledger），它在 180 次运行中 141 次达成谈判结局。论文结论不是“推理有害”，而是方法论警示：模型能力与仿真保真度是两个不同目标，必须把模型当作“采样器”而非仅“求解器”单独进行资格认证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型被广泛用作社会、经济与政策仿真中的代理人（合成受访者、谈判对手、政策协商中的利益相关方）。这类应用的隐含前提是：更强的推理能力应当带来更高的仿真保真度。论文质疑这一前提。当仿真目标不是求解一个战略问题，而是从“有界理性人可能做什么”的分布中采样时，推理增强的模型会变成更好的求解器、更差的采样器：它们为占优策略过度优化、压缩以妥协为导向的终局行为，甚至出现“无多样性但无保真”的模式——局部变异保留而结果层保真崩溃。既有证据（后训练收窄输出分布、推理算力不提升仿真保真）均为单智能体静态提示下的研究，缺乏对交互式多智能体场景的检验。

Method: 构建三个共享同一协议的多方谈判环境（E1 碎片化权威的华尔街式交易限制、E2 行业统一阵线、E3 电网限电新领域），五方智能体最多谈判15轮，未决条款由常设机构（当局）裁决；每轮从 {Support, Oppose, Concede, Counter, Exit} 闭集动作集中选一个并给出简短理由。设置三种条件：None（基线，无私有反思）、Ledger（要求智能体每轮覆写五个私有字段：我的让步/对方让步/当前状态/对手评估/未决议题）、Native（开启厂商自带推理模式，保持相同公开输出格式）。主矩阵为 Gemini 3.1 与 DeepSeek V3.2 两个族 × 3 环境 × 3 条件 × 15 次 = 270 次，扩展加入 GPT-4.1 与 GPT-5.2 共 225 次，构成 495 次结果矩阵，另有约 600 次对照运行，总计约 1150 次。测量以“运行”为分析单元：结局分布（妥协/共识/当局裁决，由确定性分类器仅读公开动作）、归一化动作熵、期限耗尽率，辅助指标为让步弧线与轨迹唯一性。提出双向可信性筛查（基于 Wilson 置信界的两侧 plausibility screen）、完整给出的确定性结局分类器与动作解析器（含动作出处审计与“最坏情况重编码”认证规则）。统计上用单侧 Fisher 精确检验比较 Ledger vs Native 的达成数，熵与耗尽率用双侧置换检验并 Holm 校正，附 Cliff's δ 与自助法 95% CI。对照包括：token 预算匹配、泛化记忆、自由形式 scratchpad、只读状态摘要、温度扫描、Gemini 推理强度阶梯、去除“让步”措辞的重标记、分类器阈值扫描、场景变体，以及五个手写有界理性策略（时间型让步者、含噪让步者、混合总体、满意即止者、互惠者、强硬派）的离线基线。

Result: 1）无账本配置几乎从不达成协议：None 与 Native 在四个模型族、三环境中 315 次运行中 314 次以当局裁决告终；Native 全部 135 次、None 全部 180 次（除 1 次 GPT-4.1 E1 妥协）均无协议。2）开启厂商原生推理对结局分布的改变恰好为零（None 与 Native 两行完全相同），说明坍缩先于推理发生。3）Ledger 在 180 次运行中 141 次达成谈判结局，六个主对比 Fisher 检验 p ≤ 8.8×10⁻⁷；GPT-4.1 多数达成（9–13/15），GPT-5.2 较弱（5–13/15）。4）关键解耦：DeepSeek Native 在 E3 的多样性最高（Ĥ=0.54，落在 Ledger 的 0.38–0.63 区间内）、让步弧线率 0.93、15 条轨迹各不相同，却 15/15 无协议；GPT-5.2 Native 则每格 15 次运行仅产生一条动作序列（全部 225 轮 Counter），即“求解器式”极端。5）该失败不可归因于解析伪影：回退轮次占比 ≤5.3%，非真实轮次 ≤8.9%，GPT-5.2 解析完美却坍缩最严重；最坏情况重编码下结果不变。6）12 项对照逐一排除竞争解释：token 预算、泛化记忆、自由反思、只读访问、温度、标签词汇、分类器阈值、批次偶然性均不产生差异，唯一有效因素是模型自行撰写谈判结构化状态。7）手写策略基线显示环境本身允许达成（时间型让步者 14/15、含噪 13/15、混合 13/15、满意即止 7/15、互惠者与强硬派 0/15），且两种 LLM 失败签名分别对应硬编码强硬派与互惠者。8）可信性筛查双向生效：None/Native 从“普遍不达成”一侧失败（每格 Wilson 下界 0.80，合并 0.97），Ledger 从“普遍妥协”一侧失败（合并妥协率下界 0.82）。

Conclusion: 模型能力与仿真保真度是不同的属性，各有各的检验方法；在交互式多智能体仿真中二者的背离表现为涌现出的结局分布坍缩，这是任何单次响应基准或过程级多样性测量（熵、让步活动、轨迹唯一性）都无法看见的——推理恰好恢复了这些表面指标，构成伪装。作者提出的实践建议是一套筛查纪律而非模型排名：在信任仿真的结局分布之前，以置信界表述的双向 plausibility screen 对“模型+条件”组合进行资格认证，对动作出处做最坏情况重编码审计，并把求解器强度视为不带来采样保真度任何推论的独立轴。核心贡献被定位为现象刻画、机制隔离（协商结构化记忆）与测量配方三点，边界明确：不做跨厂商推理强度比较，不宣称环境真实，不把 Ledger 作为拟真修复方案，样本量仅支持近类别对比，且缺乏人类校准数据。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used as agents in social, economic, and policy simulations. A common assumption is that stronger reasoning should improve simulation fidelity. We argue that this assumption can fail when the objective is not to solve a strategic problem, but to sample plausible boundedly rational behavior. In such settings, reasoning-enhanced models can become better solvers and worse simulators: they can over-optimize for strategically dominant actions, collapse compromise-oriented terminal behavior, and sometimes exhibit a diversity-without-fidelity pattern in which local variation survives without outcome-level fidelity. We study this solver-sampler mismatch in three multi-agent negotiation environments adapted from earlier simulation work: an ambiguous fragmented-authority trading-limits scenario, an ambiguous unified-opposition trading-limits scenario, and a new-domain grid-curtailment case in emergency electricity management. We compare three reflection conditions, no reflection, bounded reflection, and native reasoning, across two primary model families and then extend the same protocol to direct OpenAI runs with GPT-4.1 and GPT-5.2. Across all three experiments, bounded reflection produces substantially more diverse and compromise-oriented trajectories than either no reflection or native reasoning. In the direct OpenAI extension, GPT-5.2 native ends in authority decisions in 45 of 45 runs across the three experiments, while GPT-5.2 bounded recovers compromise outcomes in every environment. The contribution is not a claim that reasoning is generally harmful. It is a methodological warning: model capability and simulation fidelity are different objectives, and behavioral simulation should qualify models as samplers, not only as solvers.

</details>


### [148] [Polynomial Expansion Rank Adaptation: Enhancing Low-Rank Fine-Tuning with High-Order Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11841)
*Wenhao Zhang,Lin Mu,Li Ni,Peiquan Jin,Yiwen Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PERA通过在低秩因子空间中引入结构化多项式展开，将自适应空间转换为多项式流形，从而在保持推理成本不变的情况下增强表达能力。实验表明，加入高阶非线性分量（尤其是平方项）对提升表达能力和在不同秩设置下维持稳健性能至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LoRA的严格线性结构限制了表达能力，其双线性权重更新公式只能捕获低秩因子之间的一阶依赖性，无法建模非线性及高阶参数交互。

Method: PERA在组合前先扩展每个低秩因子以合成高阶交互项，将自适应空间转换为多项式流形，从而建模更丰富的非线性耦合，且不增加秩或推理成本。

Result: PERA在多个基准测试中始终优于现有最先进方法。特别地发现，加入高阶非线性分量（尤其是平方项）对于增强表达能力和在各种秩设置下保持强劲稳健性能至关重要。

Conclusion: PERA提供理论分析和实验验证：相比现有线性自适应方法具有更强的表达能力和更有效的特征利用，且无需增加推理开销。

Abstract: Low-rank adaptation (LoRA) is a widely used strategy for efficient fine-tuning of large language models (LLMs), but its strictly linear structure fundamentally limits expressive capacity. The bilinear formulation of weight updates captures only first-order dependencies between low-rank factors, restricting the modeling of nonlinear and higher-order parameter interactions. In this paper, we propose Polynomial Expansion Rank Adaptation (PERA), a novel method that introduces structured polynomial expansion directly into the low-rank factor space. By expanding each low-rank factor to synthesize high-order interaction terms before composition, PERA transforms the adaptation space into a polynomial manifold capable of modeling richer nonlinear coupling without increasing rank or inference cost. We provide theoretical analysis demonstrating that PERA offers enhanced expressive capacity and more effective feature utilization compare to existing linear adaptation approaches. Empirically, PERA consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods across diverse benchmarks. Notably, our experiments show that incorporating high-order nonlinear components particularly square terms is crucial for enhancing expressive capacity and maintaining strong and robust performance under various rank settings. Our code is available at https://github.com/zhangwenhao6/PERA

</details>


### [149] [DBGL: Decay-aware Bipartite Graph Learning for Irregular Medical Time Series Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11842)
*Jian Chen,Yuzhu Hu,Xiaoyan Yuan,Yuxuan Hu,Jinfeng Xu,Yipeng Du,Wenhao Yuan,Wei Wang,Edith C. H. Ngai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DBGL是一种用于不规则医疗时间序列建模的图学习方法，通过构建患者-变量二部图捕捉不规则采样模式，并设计节点特定的时序衰减编码机制建模变量衰减规律，在4个公开数据集上验证了优越性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在处理不规则医疗时间序列时存在两个关键问题：1) 扭曲了时间采样不规则性和缺失模式；2) 未能捕捉可变的衰减不规则性。这导致次优的表示学习效果，亟需一种能同时建模采样不规律性和变量衰减动态的方法。

Method: DBGL提出两个核心组件：1) 患者-变量二部图，同时捕捉不规则采样模式（无需人工对齐）并自适应建模变量关系；2) 节点特定的时序衰减编码机制，基于采样间隔捕获每个变量的衰减率。

Result: 在4个公开医疗数据集上的实验表明，DBGL在各项指标上均优于所有基线方法。

Conclusion: DBGL通过创新的图学习架构和衰减建模机制，能够更准确、忠实地表示不规则时间动态，为临床诊断和患者状态评估提供了更可靠的表示基础。

Abstract: Irregular Medical Time Series play a critical role in the clinical domain to better understand the patient's condition. However, inherent irregularity arising from heterogeneous sampling rates, asynchronous observations, and variable gaps poses key challenges for reliable modeling. Existing methods often distort temporal sampling irregularity and missingness patterns while failing to capture variable decay irregularity, resulting in suboptimal representations. To address these limitations, we introduce DBGL, Decay-Aware Bipartite Graph Learning for Irregular Medical Time Series. DBGL first introduces a patient-variable bipartite graph that simultaneously captures irregular sampling patterns without artificial alignment and adaptively models variable relationships for temporal sampling irregularity modeling, enhancing representation learning. To model variable decay irregularity, DBGL designs a novel node-specific temporal decay encoding mechanism that captures each variable's decay rates based on sampling interval, yielding a more accurate and faithful representation of irregular temporal dynamics. We evaluate the performance of DBGL on four publicly available datasets, and the results show that DBGL outperforms all baselines.

</details>


### [150] [Disposition Distillation at Small Scale: A Three-Arc Negative Result](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11867)
*Hari Sadasivan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究报道了一个重要的负面结果：研究团队无法通过蒸馏、注意力头干预或冻结侧车等方法在小型语言模型中稳定地注入行为倾向（自我验证、不确定性确认、反馈整合）。最初报告的显著收益被证实为假阳性（截断伪影和评分标准不一致），而系统性验证显示这些操作要么损害模型内容质量，要么仅产生表面风格模仿。跨分布泛化能力几乎为零。研究提供了详尽的失败机制分析和一个将假阳性转化为可发表负面成果的证伪流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究团队在内部初稿中声称通过蒸馏管道在Qwen3-0.6B上取得了+33.9分MCAS和+15.3分HumanEval的显著提升，但随后的二轮审查发现这些数字均为假阳性，需要系统性地证伪并深入调查失败原因。

Method: 采用四阶段全MIT蒸馏管道在0.6B至2.3B参数规模的小型语言模型上训练三种行为倾向（自我验证、不确定性确认、反馈整合），并在三个后续方向进行验证：(1) 三个模型家族和两个领域上的SFT/DPO LoRA；(2) 对o_proj的推理时注意力头 tempering；(3) 读取最终令牌隐藏状态h_last的无训练冻结基础侧车。内部初稿使用了MCAS和HumanEval作为评估指标。

Result: 所有实验均未找到能在不损害内容或陷入风格模仿的情况下改变LLM行为倾向的操作符：(1) HumanEval收益实际上是截断伪影，在n_predict=1024时反转为-8.0分；(2) MCAS收益在统一评分标准下消失；(3) 五种模型的跨分布验证从AUC=0.683崩溃至随机猜测水平（0.516）；(4) Gemma 4 E2B在Chef领域表现出置信度与正确性近乎完全解耦。

Conclusion: 尽管采用了多种技术手段（SFT/DPO LoRA、注意力头干预、冻结基础侧车），研究团队在5个模型上均未找到能够在不损害内容质量或陷入风格模仿的情况下改变LLM行为倾向的操作符。跨分布泛化能力从AUC=0.683跌至随机水平（0.516）。本研究提供了带有机制解释的三弧线负面结果、线性h_last探测器的双失效模式分类，以及将自身产生的假阳性转化为可发表负面结果的诚实证伪流程。Gemma 4 E2B在Chef领域展现出近乎完全的置信度-正确性解耦（断言对称性-0.009，模型以91%的置信度进行断言，与正确性无关）。

Abstract: We set out to train behavioral dispositions (self-verification, uncertainty acknowledgment, feedback integration) into small language models (0.6B to 2.3B effective parameters) through a four-stage all-MIT distillation pipeline, with follow-on experiments on inference-time attention-head interventions and a frozen-base confidence-gated sidecar. An internal draft reported +33.9-point MCAS and +15.3-point HumanEval gains on a Qwen3-0.6B student; a second-pass sanity check falsified both numbers before publication. The HumanEval delta was a truncation artifact (n_predict=512) that inverted to -8.0 points at n_predict=1024; the MCAS gain disappeared under apples-to-apples scoring. That falsification triggered three subsequent arcs. Across (1) SFT/DPO LoRA on three model families and two domains, (2) inference-time attention-head tempering on o_proj, and (3) a training-free frozen-base sidecar reading the final-token hidden state h_last, we find no operator that moves judge-measured disposition without damaging content or collapsing into stylistic mimicry. The failure is consistent across five models (Qwen3-0.6B, Qwen3-1.7B, Qwen3.5-0.8B, Gemma 4 E2B, and SmolLM2-1.7B-Instruct). A within-distribution cross-validation pass (AUC=0.683) collapsed to chance on fresh prompts (AUC=0.516). We contribute a three-arc negative result with mechanism, a two-failure-mode taxonomy for linear h_last probes, and an honest falsification pipeline that converts the class of false positives we ourselves produced into publishable negatives. As an independent finding, Gemma 4 E2B exhibits near-complete confidence-correctness decoupling on the Chef domain (assertion asymmetry -0.009; the model asserts at 91% regardless of correctness).

</details>


### [151] [Subcritical Signal Propagation at Initialization in Normalization-Free Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11890)
*Sergey Alekseev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将 Doshi et al. (2023) 的平均偏雅可比范数 (APJN) 框架推广到带双向注意力、置换对称 token 配置的 Transformer，导出激活协方差与 APJN 的逐层递推关系，并证明：用 tanh 类逐元素非线性 (DyT/Derf) 替换 LayerNorm 后，APJN 呈拉伸指数 (stretched-exponential) 增长，属"次临界 (subcritical)"区；而 pre-LN 保持幂律增长的"临界"区。理论预测与深度 ViT 实测 APJN 吻合（中深层 GMFE 通常 < 1.25），并解释了为何 Derf 对初始化尺度、深度和 α 更敏感、需精细调参。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期工作 (Zhu et al. 2025; Chen et al. 2025) 用逐元素 tanh 类非线性（Dynamic Tanh (DyT)、Dynamic erf (Derf)）替换 Transformer 中的 LayerNorm，动机是二者输入-输出曲线相似。这类"normalization-free"架构提出一个自然问题：把 LayerNorm 换成饱和型逐元素非线性后，初始化时的信号传播（signal propagation）与训练稳定性如何变化？已有的关键性 (criticality) 理论表明，残差网络 pre-LN 可在任意权重初始化下达到临界，梯度按幂律随深度增长；而饱和非线性在残差网络中已知会导致次临界 (stretched-exponential) 梯度放大（Yang & Schoenholz 2017; Doshi et al. 2023），但 Transformer + 注意力情形缺少系统分析。此外，post-LN 会出现梯度指数消失（Kedia et al. 2024），故需定量刻画注意力对 APJN 渐近行为（尺度参数与临界指数）的修正。

Method: 理论与实验两部分。(1) 理论：设架构为 L=2B 层（偶数层注意力、奇数层 ReLU MLP），残差分支输入为 LayerNorm 或 φ_α(h)=tanh(αh)/erf(αh)（初始化时 γ=1, β=0）。采用三条假设（Assumption 2.1）：置换对称 token 配置（层间几何由自点积 q^l 与跨位置点积 p^l 两个量刻画）、大宽度高斯平均场近似、初始化时均匀注意力近似 A_st≈1/n。据此导出协方差递推（Eq. 8–9，含 ReLU 的 κ 函数）、APJN 递推（Result 3.2 简化式与附录 C 的含 cross-positional Jacobian correlation K 的完整式，前向/后向）以及渐近分析：Q^b 线性增长，pre-LN 给出幂律 J^{b,0}~b^ζ、ζ=(½σ21²)/(½σ21²+σOV²)；tanh 类给出 J^{b,0}~b^{-1/(8λ)}e^{√(b/λ)}，λ^{-1}=C_α²σ21⁴/(½σ21²+σOV² p̃⋆)，C_α=(1/√(2π))∫φ'²；并给出 c⋆ 不动点与稳定性分析（c⋆=1 对 tanh 类不稳定）。(2) 实验：timm 的 vit_base_patch16_224（CIFAR-100，n=196, d=768），APJN 用 Hutchinson 估计器测 Jacobian Frobenius 范数；在置换对称合成输入与 CIFAR-100 真实输入上对比理论与实测后向 APJN（GMFE 指标）；并做训练稳定性实验（深度、权重初始化尺度、warmup、学习率、α 扫描），辅以梯度放大测量。

Result: (1) 理论正确复现实测：在满足假设的置换对称输入上理论与 ViT 实测后向 APJN 高度吻合；在 CIFAR-100 真实输入的 128-block ViT 上，中/深层 GMFE 通常 < 1.25，深度越大越准（因为点积涨落随深度衰减，更接近置换对称假设）。(2) 关键性图景迁移到 Transformer：pre-LN 的 APJN 幂律增长（临界），指数 ζ 由 σ21、σOV 决定；DyT/Derf 因 q̂ ~ 1/√q（对比 LayerNorm 的 1/q）呈拉伸指数增长（次临界），且 λ^{-1} 无上界，可通过权重初始化任意调陡，渐近式在深块才成立。(3) 注意力通过协方差动力学（σOV² p̃⋆、渐近跨 token 余弦 c⋆）间接改变尺度参数与临界指数；σOV 较大时 cross-positional 相关项不可忽略（给出完整递推）。(4) 训练稳定性：Derf 比 pre-LN 对深度与权重初始化尺度更敏感，存在临界深度，超过则不收敛（α 越大边界越浅）；pre-LN 在同等初始化下全深度范围收敛率近似恒定。Derf 在较大 α 时可通过降低学习率、延长 warmup 缓解；较小 α 可在更宽的学习率/warmup/深度范围稳定训练，但 α 过小（如 0.1）会显著拖慢收敛；冻结 α 会进一步恶化收敛。梯度放大实测与理论一致：深度 12 时 pre-LN 与 Derf(α≈0.9) 相当，深度 36 时与 Derf(α≈0.5–0.7) 相当。

Conclusion: 把 LayerNorm 换成 tanh 类逐元素非线性会使 Transformer 在初始化时从"临界"变为"次临界"信号传播：APJN 由幂律变为拉伸指数增长，且由于末尾归一化层也被替换（其梯度因子 Q̂^B 随深度、权重尺度增大而大于 pre-LN 的 1/Q^B），总梯度放大更强。因此 DyT/Derf 架构需要更谨慎地调节初始化参数（权重标准差、α）、学习率与 warmup，α 初始化是关键超参；α 的选取存在"稳定性—收敛速度"权衡。局限性：实验限于 CIFAR-100 上的 ViT 与截断训练，未评估长期动态与最终性能；理论基于均匀注意力、置换对称 token、均值场等简化假设，推广到更复杂 token 几何仍待研究；该理论只解释初始化处的信号传播与梯度放大趋势，而非完整训练动力学。

Abstract: We study signal propagation at initialization in transformers through the averaged partial Jacobian norm (APJN), a measure of gradient amplification across layers. We extend APJN analysis to transformers with bidirectional attention and permutation-symmetric input token configurations by deriving recurrence relations for activation statistics and APJNs across layers. Our theory predicts how attention modifies the asymptotic behavior of the APJN at large depth and matches APJNs measured in deep vision transformers. The criticality picture known from residual networks carries over to transformers: the pre-LayerNorm architecture exhibits power-law APJN growth, whereas transformers with LayerNorm replaced by elementwise $\tanh$-like nonlinearities have stretched-exponential APJN growth, indicating that the latter are subcritical. Applied to Dynamic Tanh (DyT) and Dynamic erf (Derf) transformers, the theory explains why these architectures can be more sensitive to initialization and optimization choices and require careful tuning for stable training.

</details>


### [152] [Thermodynamic Liquid Manifold Networks: Physics-Bounded Deep Learning for Solar Forecasting in Autonomous Off-Grid Microgrids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11909)
*Mohammed Ezzaldin Babiker Abdullah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"热力学液态流形网络"（Thermodynamic Liquid Manifold Network, TLMN），用于离网光伏系统的太阳能预测：将22个气象与几何变量映射到Koopman线性化的黎曼流形上，结合"光谱校准单元"与乘性"热力学Alpha-Gate"，把实时大气不透明度与理论晴空边界模型融合，在结构层面强制遵守太阳几何（夜间恒为零），从而消除数据驱动模型常见的"夜间幽灵发电"与云瞬变相位滞后。在半干旱气候下五年（1826天）测试中报告RMSE 18.31 Wh/m²、Pearson 0.988，夜间误差恒为0，高频光学瞬变相位响应<30分钟，模型仅63,458个可训练参数，面向边缘侧微电网控制器部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离网光伏系统的稳定运行依赖可靠的短期太阳能预测（辐照度/GHI）。现有深度学习模型存在两类关键缺陷：①在云层瞬变过程中出现严重的时序相位滞后，导致出力预测错过快速天气变化；②产生物理上不可能的夜间发电量（数据驱动的输出与太阳几何/热力学约束相冲突）。作者的核心动机是：让预测模型内生地服从确定性天体几何与大气热力学规律，而非仅靠数据拟合，从而弥合"数据驱动建模"与"确定性力学"之间的分歧，同时保持轻量以便在边缘设备上部署。

Method: 方法围绕三条技术线展开：(1) 变量空间构建——选取22个气象与几何变量（含GHI/辐照分量、气象量与太阳位置角等几何量），统一投影到经Koopman线性化处理的黎曼流形上，以表征复杂气候动态；(2) 架构模块——设置Spectral Calibration（光谱校准）单元，对实时大气光学厚度/不透明度信号进行校正；设置乘性门控Thermodynamic Alpha-Gate，以乘性方式调制隐状态/输出；(3) 物理约束机制——将实时大气不透明度信号与理论晴空边界模型（如clear-sky irradiance model）综合，使输出在结构上被限制在晴空物理包络内，夜间输出被强制归零，从机制上消除"幻影夜间发电"。整体以流形表示学习+门控+物理先验融合的方式实现，参数规模控制在63,458，属于超轻量网络。验证方案：五年测试期、1826个测试日，覆盖高频云瞬变样本，指标为RMSE（Wh/m²）、Pearson相关、相位响应时间与夜间误差幅度。

Result: 在半干旱气候的五年测试horizon（共1826天）上：RMSE = 18.31 Wh/m²，Pearson相关系数 = 0.988；所有1826天的夜间误差幅值严格为0（无夜间生成误差）；在高频光学瞬变工况下，相位响应时间小于30分钟，接近零滞后同步；模型总可训练参数63,458，可视为边缘可部署规模。作者据此宣称该框架在热力学一致性上优于当代深度学习模型，并可作为边缘微电网控制器的轻量标准。

Conclusion: 结论为：把太阳几何与晴空热力学包络作为结构性硬约束嵌入预测网络（而非后处理惩罚项），可同时获得物理合规性（夜间零输出）与动态跟踪精度（快速天气变化下近零相位滞后），在极小参数量下达到高精度，表明"物理一致性先验+轻量流形模型"是离网微电网场景中具有实用价值的路线。需注意：摘要未给出与既有基线（NWP、GBDT、LSTM/Transformer等）的同口径对比、无消融实验（各模块贡献未拆分）、未说明数据来源与划分细节与晴空模型形式，且"夜间误差严格为0"与"零滞后"属强断言，其成立高度依赖测试数据中夜间标签本身是否为精确零值及评估协议，需以原文与代码复核。整体而言，该工作思路（显式物理约束+轻量化）合理且契合领域趋势，但当前证据链尚不完整，结论强度高于所提供证据。

Abstract: The stable operation of autonomous off-grid photovoltaic systems requires solar forecasting algorithms that respect atmospheric thermodynamics. Contemporary deep learning models consistently exhibit critical anomalies, primarily severe temporal phase lags during cloud transients and physically impossible nocturnal power generation. To resolve this divergence between data-driven modeling and deterministic celestial mechanics, this research introduces the Thermodynamic Liquid Manifold Network. The methodology projects 22 meteorological and geometric variables into a Koopman-linearized Riemannian manifold to systematically map complex climatic dynamics. The architecture integrates a Spectral Calibration unit and a multiplicative Thermodynamic Alpha-Gate. This system synthesizes real-time atmospheric opacity with theoretical clear-sky boundary models, structurally enforcing strict celestial geometry compliance. This completely neutralizes phantom nocturnal generation while maintaining zero-lag synchronization during rapid weather shifts. Validated against a rigorous five-year testing horizon in a severe semi-arid climate, the framework achieves an RMSE of 18.31 Wh/m2 and a Pearson correlation of 0.988. The model strictly maintains a zero-magnitude nocturnal error across all 1826 testing days and exhibits a sub-30-minute phase response during high-frequency optical transients. Comprising exactly 63,458 trainable parameters, this ultra-lightweight design establishes a robust, thermodynamically consistent standard for edge-deployable microgrid controllers.

</details>


### [153] [A Residual-Shell-Based Lower Bound for Ollivier-Ricci Curvature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12211)
*Xiang Gu,Huichun Zhang,Jian Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于"残差壳层"(residual-shell)耦合的 Ollivier–Ricci 曲率(ORC)下界(RS-LB):将最优传输按图距离壳层逐层分解,优先在低代价近邻壳层内匹配尽可能多的质量,再以可控代价完成残余质量传输,从而给出 Wasserstein 距离的显式上界。该界比已有 1-跳下界(Kang & Park, KP-LB)紧得多,同时比线性规划精确计算 ORC 快数十倍,并且天然适用于 k-跳(k>1)随机游走定义的 ORC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Ollivier–Ricci 曲率通过最优传输的 Wasserstein 距离刻画图的几何结构,应用广泛(网络差异度量、瓶颈检测、异常检测等),但精确计算 W1 需要求解线性规划,时间开销极大,难以扩展到大规模图。已有替代方案 Jost & Liu(连续度量空间)、Kang & Park(离散带惰性整数度量空间)给出的下界虽可高效计算,却存在两大缺陷:(1) 与真实 ORC 的差距很大,近似误差显著;(2) 仅适用于 1-跳随机游走,无法处理 k>1 跳的 ORC 定义。因此需要一个既更紧、又适用于一般 k-跳、同时计算代价远低于精确 LP 的 ORC 下界。

Method: 1) 预备:定义加权随机游走转移矩阵 P、k-跳惰性测度 μ_x^k=(1-α)P^k(x,·)+αδ_x,以及 ORC κ(x,y)=1-W1(μ_x^k,μ_y^k)/d(x,y);壳层集合 E_r={(u,v)∈N_k(x)×N_k(y): d(u,v)=r}。2) 残差壳层耦合构造:初始化残余边际 a_u=μ_x^k(u), b_v=μ_y^k(v),对每个壳层 r 依次分配 f_uv^(r)=min{a_u,b_v},并更新残余边际,累计壳层质量 m_r 与残余质量 R_l=1-Σm_r。3) 定理 3.1:若残余支撑对 (u,v) 满足 d(u,v)≤r̄,则 W1(μ_x^k,μ_y^k) ≤ Σ_{r≤l} r·m_r + r̄·R_l,进而 κ(x,y) ≥ 1-(Σ r·m_r + r̄·R_l)/d(x,y)。证明通过构造可行耦合 π=π_{≤l}+R_l·γ(π_{≤l} 由壳层分配构成,γ 为归一化残余测度间的任意耦合)并做代价分解。4) 算法 1:给出逐壳层贪心分配的线性级实现,取 l=3,r̄=max{d(u,v): a(u)>0,b(v)>0};固定 r̄ 时界对 l 单调不增,l 越大越紧。方法仅依赖最短路距离,不需环计数,对不同环长的图均适用。

Result: 在 ER、BA、WS 随机图、三角/方/六边形平面网格,以及真实数据集 Amazon(10224 节点/175608 边)、Facebook(1081 节点/27552 边)及其特征高斯核加权版本(WAm、WFB)上评测,与精确 ORC(LP 求解)和 KP-LB 对比:(1) 运行时间上,RS-LB 虽比 KP-LB 稍慢,但相对精确 ORC 取得数十倍加速;(2) 近似精度上,RS-LB 的平均绝对间隙显著小于 KP-LB,其曲率直方图与精确 ORC 更吻合,在 Grid 图上 RS-LB 与精确 ORC 完全一致且耗时更短;(3) 扩展到 k-跳(k>1)随机游走(KP-LB 不适用)时,RS-LB 近似误差低于 0.05,并在不同 k 下保持 10~20 倍加速。

Conclusion: 本文提出的残差壳层下界 RS-LB 在保持远低于精确 ORC 计算成本的同时,显著收紧了已有的 1-跳下界 KP-LB,并把适用性推广到一般 k-跳惰性随机游走定义的 ORC,为大规模图上的曲率分析提供了理论保证且高效的替代量。作者指出未来工作将探索 RS-LB 在更多下游任务(如异常检测、瓶颈识别)中的应用;代码开源于 https://github.com/XJTU-XGU/ORC_bound。局限:目前仅在合成图与两个真实数据集上验证,下界的紧密度与 l、r̄ 的选取密切相关,更紧的最优壳层策略仍有探索空间。

Abstract: Ollivier-Ricci curvature (ORC), defined via the Wasserstein distance that captures rich geometric information, has received growing attention in both theory and applications. However, the high computational cost of Wasserstein distance evaluation has significantly limited the broader practical use of ORC. To alleviate this issue, previous work introduced a computationally efficient lower bound as a proxy for ORC based on 1-hop random walks, but this approach empirically exhibits large gaps from the exact ORC. In this paper, we establish a substantially tighter lower bound for ORC than the existing lower bound, while retaining much lower computational cost than exact ORC computation, with practical speedups of tens of times. Moreover, our bound is not restricted to 1-hop random walks, but also applies to k-hop random walks (k > 1). Experiments on several fundamental graph structures demonstrate the effectiveness of our bound in terms of both approximation accuracy and computational efficiency.

</details>


### [154] [How Transformers Learn to Plan via Multi-Token Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11912)
*Jianhao Huang,Zhanpeng Zhou,Renqiu Xia,Baharan Mirzasoleiman,Weijie Su,Wei Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究多词元预测（MTP）相比传统单词元预测（NTP）在推理任务中的优势。实验表明MTP在图路径搜索、Countdown和SAT问题上表现更优。理论分析揭示MTP在星图任务上诱导两阶段反向推理：首先关注终点节点，然后反向追踪中间节点重建路径。这一机制源于MTP的梯度解耦特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统单词元预测（NTP）在捕捉全局结构方面存在不足，尤其在推理任务中表现欠佳。多词元预测（MTP）虽被视为有潜力的替代方案，但其内在机制尚不清晰。

Method: 实验方面：在合成图路径搜索任务和真实推理基准（Countdown、布尔可满足性问题）上进行评估。理论方面：在星图任务上对简化双层Transformer进行数学分析。

Result: MTP在所有任务上持续优于NTP。理论分析证明MTP诱导两阶段反向推理过程：首先关注终点节点，然后反向重建路径。MTP具有梯度解耦特性，提供比NTP更清晰的训练信号。

Conclusion: 多词元目标函数本身会引导优化过程形成稳健且可解释的推理电路，为理解MTP的机制提供了理论支撑。

Abstract: While next-token prediction (NTP) has been the standard objective for training language models, it often struggles to capture global structure in reasoning tasks. Multi-token prediction (MTP) has recently emerged as a promising alternative, yet its underlying mechanisms remain poorly understood. In this paper, we study how MTP facilitates reasoning, with a focus on planning. Empirically, we show that MTP consistently outperforms NTP on both synthetic graph path-finding tasks and more realistic reasoning benchmarks, such as Countdown and boolean satisfiability problems. Theoretically, we analyze a simplified two-layer Transformer on a star graph task. We prove that MTP induces a two-stage reverse reasoning process: the model first attends to the end node and then reconstructs the path by tracing intermediate nodes backward. This behavior arises from a gradient decoupling property of MTP, which provides a cleaner training signal compared to NTP. Ultimately, our results highlight how multi-token objectives inherently bias optimization toward robust and interpretable reasoning circuits.

</details>


### [155] [Can AI Detect Life? Lessons from Artificial Life](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11915)
*Ankit Gupta,Christoph Adami*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究利用人工生命(Artificial Life)实验证明，现有的机器学习生命检测方法容易被"分布外样本"欺骗，在分析真实地外样本时可能产生高达近100%的假阳性率，揭示了AI生命检测技术的重大隐患。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation:  extraterrestrial samples are inherently outside the distribution of training data (terrestrial biotic and abiotic samples), making modern ML-based life detection methods vulnerable to false positives. The research aims to investigate whether current ML approaches can reliably detect extraterrestrial life.

Method: The researchers used Artificial Life (ALife) techniques to create synthetic samples that are definitely non-living but chemically complex, then tested whether state-of-the-art ML classifiers could distinguish these from actual living systems.

Result: ML methods were easily fooled, detecting "life" with near 100% confidence even in samples that were provably incapable of sustaining life. The high false positive rate stems from ML's tendency to overfit to patterns in training data that don't generalize to truly novel chemical systems.

Conclusion: AI-based life detection methods are fundamentally unreliable for extraterrestrial samples unless training data can be expanded to include a representative distribution of non-terrestrial chemistry. Researchers caution against deploying such methods in actual space missions without addressing this out-of-distribution vulnerability.

Abstract: Modern machine learning methods have been proposed to detect life in extraterrestrial samples, drawing on their ability to distinguish biotic from abiotic samples based on training models using natural and synthetic organic molecular mixtures. Here we show using Artificial Life that such methods are easily fooled into detecting life with near 100% confidence even if the analyzed sample is not capable of life. This is due to modern machine learning methods' propensity to be easily fooled by out-of-distribution samples. Because extra-terrestrial samples are very likely out of the distribution provided by terrestrial biotic and abiotic samples, using AI methods for life detection is bound to yield significant false positives.

</details>


### [156] [Is Sliding Window All You Need? An Open Framework for Long-Sequence Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12372)
*Sayak Chakrabarty,Souradip Pal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明长序列训练在学术规模下不仅可行且有效。提出k-shift embedding layer实现百万级词表在普通GPU上运行，开发完整端到端框架，在Retailrocket数据集上MRR提升6.04%、Recall@10提升6.34%，训练时间增加约4倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代推荐系统依赖长交互历史，但长序列训练因内存和延迟限制被认为不切实际。本文旨在证明长序列训练不仅可行且有效。

Method: 发布完整端到端框架（含数据处理、训练、评估脚本）；提出k-shift embedding layer支持百万级词表；进行运行时感知消融研究量化精度-计算权衡。

Result: Retailrocket数据集：MRR +6.04%，Recall@10 +6.34%；训练时间约4倍开销；可在普通大学集群运行。

Conclusion: 将长序列训练从工业封闭技术转化为开源、可扩展的社区方法，提升推荐系统研究的可复现性和可访问性。

Abstract: Long interaction histories are central to modern recommender systems, yet training with long sequences is often dismissed as impractical under realistic memory and latency budgets. This work demonstrates that it is not only practical but also effective-at academic scale. We release a complete, end-to-end framework that implements industrial-style long-sequence training with sliding windows, including all data processing, training, and evaluation scripts. Beyond reproducing prior gains, we contribute two capabilities missing from earlier reports: (i) a runtime-aware ablation study that quantifies the accuracy-compute frontier across windowing regimes and strides, and (ii) a novel k-shift embedding layer that enables million-scale vocabularies on commodity GPUs with negligible accuracy loss. Our implementation trains reliably on modest university clusters while delivering competitive retrieval quality (e.g., up to +6.04% MRR and +6.34% Recall@10 on Retailrocket) with $\sim 4 \times $ training-time overheads. By packaging a robust pipeline, reporting training time costs, and introducing an embedding mechanism tailored for low-resource settings, we transform long-sequence training from a closed, industrial technique into a practical, open, and extensible methodology for the community.

</details>


### [157] [Identifying and Mitigating Gender Cues in Academic Recommendation Letters: An Interpretability Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12337)
*Charlotte S. Alexander,Shane Storks,Souradip Pal,Sayak Chakrabarty,Arushi Sharma,Mlen-Too Wesley,Bailey Russo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文以美国某大型麻醉学住院医项目 8,992 封推荐信（LoR）为语料，将"申请人性别推断"建模为文本分类任务，检验在姓名、代词、称谓、亲属称谓等显式性别标识被去除后，Transformer 编码器与大模型能否仅凭隐含语言线索反推性别。作者用 DistilBERT、RoBERTa、Llama 2（LoRA）微调，实现三阶段流水线：语料构建→显式去性别化（EDG）→基于 TF-IDF/SHAP 的可解释性审计与隐式去性别化再训练。结果：非去性别化基线几乎完美（Acc 0.999 / Macro F1 0.999）；显式去性别化后仍达 63.3% 准确率、57.7% Macro F1（RoBERTa 61.0%/55.3%，Llama 2 61.2%/56.1%），远高于随机基线，说明推荐信中残留大量性别代理词；进一步屏蔽 SHAP/TF-IDF 选出的隐式线索词，DistilBERT Macro F1 仅下降约 2.7%/1.4%（降至 0.550/0.563），性别仍可高于随机地预测。作者据此主张对"上游"评价文本做性别线索审计，作为模型层公平性干预的必要补充。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推荐信（LoR）在招生/招聘等高利害评估中普遍存在且影响巨大，但其叙事性文本留有大量隐式刻板印象空间。先前工作已证明仅删除姓名、代词等"显式"性别线索不足以阻断偏置（隐式性别化语言普遍存在），且此类语言会系统性影响决策；同时 NLP/LLM 本身也会习得并复制语料中的社会刻板印象，危及高利害选择场景的公平性。但既有针对 LoR 的研究多停留在描述性/相关性分析，未对识别出的线索施加干预、也未评估移除后模型行为的变化；且多数偏置审计依赖数百份文档的人工编码，难以覆盖机构级全量语料。因此本文提出核心问题：在显式性别标识被移除后还残留多少性别信号？能否在数据集层面消除这一隐式信号？其现实背景是数字化投递平台每年归档数万封推荐信，为规模化偏置审计提供了前所未有的机会。

Method: 数据：8,992 封推荐信（2,787 封女性、6,205 封男性，约 31%/69%，性别为自报且来自官方元数据），申请人姓名替换为 FIRST_NAME/MIDDLE_NAME/LAST_NAME/IDENTIFIER 等特殊标记；分词用 BERT 方案，最大长度 L=512；80:10:10 分层划分。

显式去性别化（EDG）：整合 Bias-BERT 与 GN-GloVe 两份公开性别词表（含 he/she、Mr./Ms.、husband/father/brother、actor/actress、fraternity/sorority 等），用正则捕获复数、时态、缩写、标点与句内位置等变体，将所有显式男性词替换为其女性对应词。关键设计选择：不使用 they/them 等中立占位符（会破坏语法结构、引入人工痕迹），统一转为女性形式以保持语言流畅性，同时阻断显式线索。

模型：DistilBERT（仅解冻最后两层编码器）、RoBERTa、Llama 2-7B（LoRA PEFT，r=16、α=48、dropout=0.15 插入注意力与前馈层）。三者均以 [CLS] 隐状态接可训练仿射头 + sigmoid 输出。DistilBERT/RoBERTa 用 HF Trainer，Llama 2 用 PEFT；Adam（β1=0.9, β2=0.999, ε=1e-8）+ 线性学习率调度；网格搜索 batch size {4,16,32}、lr [1e-5, 3.7e-5]、weight decay {0, 0.03}，以验证集 Macro F1 选优；Intel Xeon Gold + A100 40GB。

可解释性：SHAP 逐 token 归因（DistilBERT/RoBERTa 全量、Llama 随机 100 封，仅保留出现≥20 次的 token，按词性分组取 top-10）；TF-IDF 按性别聚合为两篇文档提取区分性词。隐式去性别化：对每个性别、每个词性、每种方法各取 top-10 token，在 EDG 文本上替换为 mask token（Llama 2 用 unk），形成 "EDG w/o SHAP Tokens" 与 "EDG w/o TF-IDF Tokens" 两个数据集并重训三模型。

预测翻转分析：仅用 DistilBERT，选取"EDG 上正确分类但在隐式去性别化模型上被误分类"的子集，对多数类随机下采样并多次重复取平均，统计各 token 替换后 F→M / M→F 的翻转次数。附录 B 另做 BERTopic 主题建模（251 个主题，二值主题向量 + 随机森林分类，并与 DistilBERT 嵌入拼接融合），用 SHAP 汇总图解释主题级贡献。

Result: 1) 显式线索可被有效去除：非 EDG 原文 DistilBERT Acc 0.999、Macro F1 0.999，说明"he/Ms."等表层线索几乎决定性别；EDG 后 DistilBERT Acc 0.633 / Macro F1 0.577，RoBERTa 0.610/0.553，Llama 2 0.612/0.561，且多数类（男）Precision 0.72–0.73、少数类（女）Precision 仅约 0.39–0.41，摘要概括为最高 68% 分类准确率。

2) 隐式性别线索清晰可辨：女性关联词包括 humanitarian、delightful、wonderful、children、compassionate、health、research、student、applicant、patient；男性关联词包括 respectful、military、combat、humble、medicine、staff、physician、leadership、rotation、show、believe、supervise。TF-IDF 差异（如 female: compassionate −0.0043, warm, outstanding；male: calm +0.0105, professional, ethic）呈系统性。词替换翻转统计印证方向性："leadership" 替换后 M→F 翻转 9 次而反向仅 3 次；"rotation" 与 "liked" 更常引起 F→M 翻转。

3) 隐式去性别化效果有限：DistilBERT 去除 SHAP token 后 Macro F1 0.577→0.550（↓约2.7%）、去除 TF-IDF token 后 →0.563（↓约1.4%），Acc 降至 0.597/0.610，仍高于随机；RoBERTa 分别 ↓0.3% 与 ↓2.3%；Llama 2 出现 Macro F1 大幅下滑（0.561→0.408/0.407，↓约15.3%/15.4%）而 Acc 反升至 0.690/0.685 的现象，反映用 unknown token 替换破坏了语义与少数类召回、模型退化为多数类偏置。

4) 主题层面的可识别性：仅用 251 维 BERTopic 主题向量的随机森林已达 Acc 0.580 / Macro F1 0.550；主题向量与 DistilBERT 嵌入拼接后 Acc 0.630 / Macro F1 0.580，说明宏观叙述主题（如家庭/儿童 vs 军事/临床实务）同样携带性别信息。

Conclusion: 结论与主张：(1) 推荐信中编码了难以根除的性别识别线索——即便统一转换为女性形式移除全部显式代词/称谓/亲属词并进一步屏蔽 SHAP/TF-IDF 定位的隐式词表，模型仍能以高于随机的方式推断性别（Acc 约 60%，Macro F1 约 0.55–0.58）；消融 LoRA 微调与遮蔽实验表明仅 1.4%–2.7% 的性能增益可归因于可解释的隐式词项，其余泄露来自更深层的语义与主题结构。(2) 本文的贡献是一个可复现的检测与干预框架（数据处理脚本 + 注意力分类基线 + 显式/隐式去性别化标注协议），可为更公平的评价流程提供具体技术步骤，例如将去性别化/性别化语言程度作为提示标记供人工评审者参考。(3) 作者同时提出批判性立场：纯技术的"去性别化"可能把候选人的身份简化为可开关的属性，抹去边缘化背景候选人的重要经历与正向特质，绕过或外包本应由机构承担的 DEI 工作，因此不可将技术方案视为终局解，必须与多元评审者、隐性偏见培训与制度改革并行；并呼吁把"上游"评价文本审计作为模型层公平性干预的必要互补。(4) 局限：遮蔽/替换破坏语义完整性与语法连贯性，仅适用于机器评估；SHAP 将子词折叠回整词与模型内部处理不完全对齐，归因存在歧义；缺少申请者完整档案与最终遴选结果数据，无法关联语言与录取结局，故定位为技术性案例研究而非因果证据；数据经 IRB 批准并已匿名化。

Abstract: Letters of recommendation (LoRs) can carry patterns of implicitly gendered language that can inadvertently influence downstream decisions, e.g. in hiring and admissions. In this work, we investigate the extent to which Transformer-based encoder models as well as Large Language Models (LLMs) can infer the gender of applicants in academic LoRs submitted to an U.S. medical-residency program after explicit identifiers like names and pronouns are de-gendered. While using three models (DistilBERT, RoBERTa, and Llama 2) to classify the gender of anonymized and de-gendered LoRs, significant gender leakage was observed as evident from up to 68% classification accuracy. Text interpretation methods, like TF-IDF and SHAP, demonstrate that certain linguistic patterns are strong proxies for gender, e.g. "emotional'' and "humanitarian'' are commonly associated with LoRs from female applicants. As an experiment in creating truly gender-neutral LoRs, these implicit gender cues were remove resulting in a drop of up to 5.5% accuracy and 2.7% macro $F_1$ score on re-training the classifiers. However, applicant gender prediction still remains better than chance. In this case study, our findings highlight that 1) LoRs contain gender-identifying cues that are hard to remove and may activate bias in decision-making and 2) while our technical framework may be a concrete step toward fairer academic and professional evaluations, future work is needed to interrogate the role that gender plays in LoR review. Taken together, our findings motivate upstream auditing of evaluative text in real-world academic letters of recommendation as a necessary complement to model-level fairness interventions.

</details>


### [158] [INTARG: Informed Real-Time Adversarial Attack Generation for Time-Series Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11928)
*Gamze Kirman Tokgoz,Onat Gungor,Tajana Rosing,Baris Aksanli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对时间序列预测模型提出了一个在线有界缓冲区设置下的对抗攻击框架，通过选择性攻击策略在模型高置信度和最大预期预测误差的时间步进行攻击，实现更少但更有效的攻击效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列预测在现实中广泛应用，但深度学习模型易受对抗攻击。现有的先进攻击方法难以直接应用于时间序列场景，因为存储完整历史数据或在每个时间步执行攻击往往不切实际。

Method: 提出一种知情且选择性的攻击策略：在模型表现出高置信度且预期预测误差最大的时间步进行选择性攻击，而非对所有时间步发起攻击。

Result: 实验表明，该框架可将预测误差提升至2.42倍，同时仅在不到10%的时间步执行攻击。

Conclusion: 该选择性攻击框架通过精准定位关键时间步，实现了攻击效率与效果的最优平衡，证明时间序列预测模型即使在稀疏攻击下仍存在显著脆弱性。

Abstract: Time-series forecasting aims to predict future values by modeling temporal dependencies in historical observations. It is a critical component of many real-world systems, where accurate forecasts improve operational efficiency and help mitigate uncertainty and risk. More recently, machine learning (ML), and especially deep learning (DL)-based models, have gained widespread adoption for time-series forecasting, but they remain vulnerable to adversarial attacks. However, many state-of-the-art attack methods are not directly applicable in time-series settings, where storing complete historical data or performing attacks at every time step is often impractical. This paper proposes an adversarial attack framework for time-series forecasting under an online bounded-buffer setting, leveraging an informed and selective attack strategy. By selectively targeting time steps where the model exhibits high confidence and the expected prediction error is maximal, our framework produces fewer but substantially more effective attacks. Experiments show that our framework can increase the prediction error up to 2.42x, while performing attacks in fewer than 10% of time steps.

</details>


### [159] [Fast and principled equation discovery from chaos to climate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11929)
*Yuzheng Zhang,Weizhen Li,Rui Carvalho*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Bayesian-ARGOS是一种混合框架，通过将快速频率主义筛选与聚焦贝叶斯推断相结合，实现自动方程发现与不确定性量化。该方法在计算成本上比现有方法降低两个数量级，同时在数据效率和噪声容忍度方面优于SINDy。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从噪声污染、有限观测数据中直接识别复杂系统方程是数据驱动科学的核心挑战，但现有基于稀疏回归的库方法在自动化、统计严谨性和计算效率之间存在妥协。

Method: Bayesian-ARGOS将快速频率主义筛选与聚焦贝叶斯推断相结合，实现自动方程发现与不确定性量化。与表示学习（SINDy-SHRED）集成用于高维海面温度重建。

Result: 在7个混沌系统测试中，Bayesian-ARGOS在大多数场景下优于两种最先进方法。相较SINDy：在所有系统上数据效率更高，6/7系统噪声容忍度更强。相较bootstrap-based ARGOS：计算成本降低两个数量级。概率框架支持影响分析和多重共线性检测等统计诊断。与SINDy-SHRED集成后，潜在方程有效率提高，长期预测稳定性显著改善。

Conclusion: Bayesian-ARGOS提供了一种有原则、自动化的计算高效方法，可从稀缺和噪声观测中获取可解释的governing equations，适用于从基准混沌系统到全球气候模式潜在动力学的跨尺度方程发现。

Abstract: Our ability to predict, control, and ultimately understand complex systems rests on discovering the equations that govern their dynamics. Identifying these equations directly from noisy, limited observations has therefore become a central challenge in data-driven science, yet existing library-based sparse regression methods force a compromise between automation, statistical rigor, and computational efficiency. Here we develop Bayesian-ARGOS, a hybrid framework that reconciles these demands by combining rapid frequentist screening with focused Bayesian inference, enabling automated equation discovery with principled uncertainty quantification at a fraction of the computational cost of existing methods. Tested on seven chaotic systems under varying data scarcity and noise levels, Bayesian-ARGOS outperforms two state-of-the-art methods in most scenarios. It surpasses SINDy in data efficiency for all systems and noise tolerance for six out of the seven, with a two-order-of-magnitude reduction in computational cost compared to bootstrap-based ARGOS. The probabilistic formulation additionally enables a suite of standard statistical diagnostics, including influence analysis and multicollinearity detection that expose failure modes otherwise opaque. When integrated with representation learning (SINDy-SHRED) for high dimensional sea surface temperature reconstruction, Bayesian-ARGOS increases the yield of valid latent equations with significantly improved long horizon stability. Bayesian-ARGOS thus provides a principled, automated, and computationally efficient route from scarce and noisy observations to interpretable governing equations, offering a practical framework for equation discovery across scales, from benchmark chaotic systems to the latent dynamics underlying global climate patterns.

</details>


### [160] [Multi-Worker Selection based Distributed Swarm Learning for Edge IoT with Non-i.i.d. Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.18367)
*Zhuoyu Yao,Yue Wang,Songyang Zhang,Yingshu Li,Zhipeng Cai,Zhi Tian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对边缘物联网中分布式群集学习(DSL)面临非独立同分布(non-i.i.d.)数据导致性能退化与训练发散的问题，提出了一种基于多工作者选择(选择多个而非单个最优工作者)的改进算法M-DSL。核心贡献包括:提出归一化的非i.i.d.程度度量η(融合标签比例与Wasserstein距离并做Min-Max缩放)、设计兼顾个体学习性能与数据质量的multi-worker选择机制(以加权得分θ与自适应阈值θ̄筛选工作者)、给出O(1/T)收敛界证明及通信开销分析,并在MNIST/CIFAR10上验证其收敛更快、精度更高、通信量低于FedAvg。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式群集学习(DSL)将粒子群优化(PSO)与梯度下降结合,是6G边缘计算中缓解非i.i.d.数据负面影响的重要范式,具备数据隐私、通信高效、节能、模型可扩展等优势。但在多接入边缘场景中,各边缘设备采集的数据高度异质,呈现标签分布偏斜(prior probability shift):不同工作者的边缘分布Pr_{D_i}(y)不同而条件分布Pr_{D_i}(y|x)相同,导致标准DSL训练退化、泛化差、全局模型聚合存在偏置甚至不收敛;且vanilla DSL的单最优工作者选择机制无法充分利用多用户系统的协作收益。同时,现有研究缺乏量化真实边缘数据异质性的有效度量(普遍依赖Dirichlet分布等人工合成数据的浓度参数α,无法反映真实统计差异),也缺乏数据异质性如何影响训练精度的理论指导。论文要回答两个核心问题:如何有效量化数据异质性?如何度量并缓解其对分布式学习性能的影响?

Method: 1) 非i.i.d.形式化:将数据异质性刻画为标签分布偏斜,先引入Wasserstein距离W_i衡量工作者i与全局数据集D_g的标签分布差异;发现单一WD与标签比例都无法刻画精度退化趋势(WD侧重分布差异而非标签类型平衡),故提出归一化非i.i.d.程度 η_i = Normalize(β1·|L_i|/|L_g| + β2·W_i + φ),其中β1、β2、φ分别表示标签多样性、统计分布差异与拟合残差的重要度,通过Min-Max缩放归一化;用FedAvg在α=0.001~1000下的精度做最小二乘线性回归拟合η与acc的趋势,得到R²=0.97(MNIST)、0.895(CIFAR10),并据此给出通用超参(CIFAR10: β1=0.286, β2=−0.07, φ=0.592;MNIST: β1=−0.031, β2=0.127, φ=−0.04),使η可替代生成参数α。2) M-DSL算法:以PS+50个工作者为框架,工作者本地训练后由PS基于综合得分 θ_{i,t}=τ·F_{i,t}+(1−τ)·η_i(τ=0.9)进行多工作者选择,选取满足 θ_{i,t}·s_{i,t} ≤ θ̄_{t−1}(上一轮全体工作者平均得分)的工作者集合,以最大化参与度为目标J(S_t)=max Σs_{i,t};全局模型按被选工作者模型增量平均聚合(式7),未选工作者不参与上传以节省通信与能量;局部更新采用PSO式速度项c0·v_{i,t}+c1·|w^l_{i,t}−w_{i,t}|+c2·|ḡ_{i,t}−w_{i,t}|−α∇F(w_{i,t},D_g),并用指示函数更新个体最优w^l与全局最优ḡ。3) 理论分析:假设梯度α阶Lipschitz光滑、局部梯度有界,证明当学习率α=1/L时,收敛误差被E[Φ̄]T界住,收敛率O(1/T)与标准DSL相当;通信方面,上传量从FedAvg的nC降为n·Σs_{i,t},且收敛轮数更少。4) 实验:5层CNN与ResNet18在MNIST/CIFAR10,i.i.d.、non-i.i.d.(Dirichlet α=0.5)与更真实的混合异质(20×α=0.1、15×α=0.5、10×α=1、5×α=10)三种情形下对比FedAvg、DSL、Multi-DSL(无η项)与M-DSL。

Result: 实验表明:(1) 所提非i.i.d.程度η的曲线与FedAvg在α变化下的精度退化趋势吻合良好,而WD与标签比例均与精度曲线存在明显差距,验证了度量的有效性;(2) M-DSL在i.i.d.、non-i.i.d. case I/II 下均取得比DSL与FedAvg更高的分类精度与更快的收敛速度;去掉η项的Multi-DSL虽因多工作者选择已提升收敛率与精度,但在异质场景下仍不及M-DSL,说明η度量的加入进一步增强了异质数据下的协作学习;(3) 线性回归拟合优度R²=0.97(MNIST)、0.895(CIFAR10),证明η可替代生成参数α作为异质性度量与选择依据;(4) 通信效率上,少量工作者在训练早期即可代表全体参与者的贡献,上传量为n·Σs_{i,t} < nC,且M-DSL比FedAvg所需通信轮数更少,节约无线资源、提升网络智能。

Conclusion: 本文提出面向边缘IoT非i.i.d.数据的多工作者选择分布式群集学习M-DSL:一是提出并验证了归一化非i.i.d.程度度量η,建立了数据异质性度量与DSL性能评估之间的联系,可替代Dirichlet浓度参数α作为异质性刻画手段;二是设计了同时考虑数据异质性与个体学习性能的多工作者选择策略(基于θ=τF+(1−τ)η与自适应阈值),在有限网络资源约束下提升网络智能,相较vanilla DSL的单工作者选择更充分利用协作收益;三是给出O(1/T)的收敛性理论保证与通信效率分析。综合仿真表明M-DSL在异质数据下具备更快收敛、更高精度与低于FedAvg的通信开销的优势,验证了其面向6G边缘智能部署的可行性与有效性。

Abstract: Recent advances in distributed swarm learning (DSL) offer a promising paradigm for edge Internet of Things. Such advancements enhance data privacy, communication efficiency, energy saving, and model scalability. However, the presence of non-independent and identically distributed (non-i.i.d.) data pose a significant challenge for multi-access edge computing, degrading learning performance and diverging training behavior of vanilla DSL. Further, there still lacks theoretical guidance on how data heterogeneity affects model training accuracy, which requires thorough investigation. To fill the gap, this paper first study the data heterogeneity by measuring the impact of non-i.i.d. datasets under the DSL framework. This then motivates a new multi-worker selection design for DSL, termed M-DSL algorithm, which works effectively with distributed heterogeneous data. A new non-i.i.d. degree metric is introduced and defined in this work to formulate the statistical difference among local datasets, which builds a connection between the measure of data heterogeneity and the evaluation of DSL performance. In this way, our M-DSL guides effective selection of multiple works who make prominent contributions for global model updates. We also provide theoretical analysis on the convergence behavior of our M-DSL, followed by extensive experiments on different heterogeneous datasets and non-i.i.d. data settings. Numerical results verify performance improvement and network intelligence enhancement provided by our M-DSL beyond the benchmarks.

</details>


### [161] [A unified data format for managing diabetes time-series data: DIAbetes eXchange (DIAX)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11944)
*Elliott C. Pryor,Marc D. Breton,Anas El Fathi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 DIAX（DIAbetes eXchange）——一种基于 JSON 的标准化数据格式，用于统一表示糖尿病时间序列数据（CGM、胰岛素、碳水、行为等信号）。其核心思想是"只存数据不托管数据"：开源仓库提供各数据集的转换脚本与 Python/MATLAB 分析工具，而非托管原始数据，从而在多源数据集整合、跨研究复用与机器学习建模之间提供轻量级、可扩展的"研究协调层"。目前已支持 DCLP3、DCLP5、IOBP2、PEDAP、T1Dexi、Loop 等公开数据集，覆盖逾一千万患者小时数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续血糖监测（CGM）、智能胰岛素笔与自动胰岛素输注（AID）系统产生了大量高质量的葡萄糖—胰岛素时间序列数据，已成为糖尿病预测、决策支持、个性化治疗及数字孪生建模的重要数据来源。然而不同来源数据集在时间表示（时区、采样频率）、计量单位（mg/dL vs mmol/L）、结构组织（泵输注 vs MDI 注射）上差异巨大，导致数据整合既费力又易出错，多数研究仅能用少量数据集，限制了模型的泛化性与人群多样性。此外，iCoDE-2 等临床标准化工作也揭示了设备异质性与数据抽象层级不一致的难题。作者希望提供一个可复现、可扩展、面向机器学习的统一表示层，同时避免强制时间对齐所引入的插值伪影。

Method: （1）设计统一 JSON schema：每名受试者对应一个 JSON 文件（如 subj_MyTrial_001-001.json），顶层键对应信号流（cgm、bolus、basal_rate、basal_inj、carbs、smbg、heart_rate、steps、height、weight、hba1c 等），每键包含平行的 time（ISO 8601，含或不含时区）与 value 数组；另设结构化元数据段，字段包括 unit、description、device（可选）、precision（可选）、insulin/medication（可选，如胰岛素门冬或赖脯）。cgm 为必需流，胰岛素强烈推荐，碳水等为可选，核心 schema 可扩展新增字段（如 glp1）。（2）不强制采样率与跨流时间对齐，保留原始带时间戳观测；将插值与重采样交由任务级工具按列可定制处理（回放仿真可用无空缺的 5 分钟网格，餐前/大剂量建议模型可用保留 NaN 的 15 分钟网格）。（3）语义约定：basal_rate 在下一个条目前视为恒定输注（泵），basal_inj 之间视为零基础量（MDI），且 basal_inj 不限于长效剂量，可表示任意离散胰岛素事件，故需元数据支撑。（4）实现为开源 GitHub 仓库（Center-for-Diabetes-Technology/DIAX），提供从原始数据集（含 BabelBetes 等其他格式）到 DIAX 的转换脚本、分析与可视化工具：Python 与 MATLAB 工具包，含时间对齐与滑窗、糖化指标（如 TIR）计算、个体与队列级 AGP 样式绘图（DIAX.plotCompare、DIAX.plotOutcomes），并可与 AGATA 等社区工具的 time-table 结构直接对接。（5）已完成数据集转换：DCLP3(n=125)、DCLP5(n=100)、IOBP2(n=343)、PEDAP(n=99)、T1Dexi(n=404)、Loop(n=845)，均来自 jaeb.org，合计逾 1000 万患者小时。

Result: DIAX 已交付为可用的开源标准化格式与工具链：统一 JSON schema 覆盖 CGM、大剂量、基础速率/注射、碳水、SMBG、HR、步数、人体测量等信号，附带丰富的逐信号元数据（设备类型、胰岛素制剂、单位、精度）；提供 Python 与 MATLAB 工具实现可定制重采样插值、临床血糖指标标准化计算、AGP 风格绘图与灵活时间窗切片；已支持并转换六大公开数据集（DCLP3、DCLP5、IOBP2、PEDAP、T1Dexi、Loop，n 合计约 1916 名受试者），覆盖超过 10 million patient-hours 数据，并与 iCoDE-2 等标准化努力及其他整合工具（如 BabelBetes、DiaData、MetaboNet、Diabetes Research Hub）保持兼容。由于不托管原始数据而仅分发转换脚本，可将新数据集的纳入成本降至仅需共享转换代码。作者强调：DIAX 是"转化研究资源"而非数据宿主，也不是临床数据交换格式的替代品；部分研究问题仍需回到原始数据源以获取完整上下文。

Conclusion: DIAX 提供了一个灵活、透明、面向研究的糖尿病时间序列数据协调层，通过标准化结构而非托管数据，在异构数据集之间实现了互操作性、可复现性与可扩展性。其关键差异化优势在于：(1) 保留设备类型与胰岛素制剂等细粒度元数据以支持跨数据集复现；(2) 打包 Python/MATLAB 分析与可视化工具链（血糖指标、AGP 绘图、时间对齐），无需额外工具；(3) 存储原始带时间戳观测而不强制时间对齐，避免插值伪影并兼容不规则/变采样率数据。统一格式使某一研究开发的分析代码与模型可直接迁移到其他数据集，支撑跨人群验证与更稳健的机器学习算法，同时也为社区驱动的开源协作（数据集作者贡献转换脚本、共享可视化库与分析工具）奠定基础。作者认为该工作可减少重复劳动、加速糖尿病技术领域的研究迭代与开放科学进程。

Abstract: Diabetes devices, including Continuous Glucose Monitoring (CGM), Smart Insulin Pens, and Automated Insulin Delivery systems, generate rich time-series data widely used in research and machine learning. However, inconsistent data formats across sources hinder sharing, integration, and analysis.
  We present DIAX (DIAbetes eXchange), a standardized JSON-based format for unifying diabetes time-series data, including CGM, insulin, and meal signals. DIAX promotes interoperability, reproducibility, and extensibility, particularly for machine learning applications. An open-source repository provides tools for dataset conversion, cross-format compatibility, visualization, and community contributions. DIAX is a translational resource, not a data host, ensuring flexibility without imposing data-sharing constraints.
  Currently, DIAX is compatible with other standardization efforts and supports major datasets (DCLP3, DCLP5, IOBP2, PEDAP, T1Dexi, Loop), totaling over 10 million patient-hours of data. https://github.com/Center-for-Diabetes-Technology/DIAX

</details>


### [162] [ResBM: Residual Bottleneck Models for Low-Bandwidth Pipeline Parallelism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11947)
*Alan Aboudib,Rodrigo Lopez Portillo A.,Kalei Brady,Steffen Cruz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Residual Bottleneck Model (ResBM)：一种为低带宽流水线并行"原生设计"的架构，在每个Transformer块的FFN之后插入跨流水线边界的编码器-解码器瓶颈模块，仅传输压缩激活（最高128×压缩），同时通过保留低秩恒等路径（identity path）保证端到端可训练性与收敛稳定性。在2B参数Llama-3架构、C4数据集、26B tokens训练下，128×压缩模型最终困惑度21.77，与未压缩AdamW基线21.75持平；在8×A10G、80 Mbps链路上吞吐从609 TPS提升至6795–7837 TPS（≈12.8×增益），接近10 Gbps中心化基线的7530 TPS。参数开销约3.3%（63.4M），每步级间通信从112 MiB降至896 KiB。相较Subspace Models (SM)，ResBM无需Grassmann流形优化或定制优化器，且在同等压缩率下性能更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练前沿大模型依赖数据并行与流水线并行，两者均依赖NVLink/InfiniBand等超高带宽互连（数据中心约300 Gb/s），而消费级互联网仅约1 Gb/s，差距约300×，导致去中心化训练中流水线并行成为核心瓶颈。去中心化数据并行已较成熟（DeMo、DiLoCo等可降低同步开销最高500×），但PP阶段间激活与梯度传输仍无有效方案。已有激活压缩方法存在明显缺陷：8-bit量化相对半精度仅2×收益；在Transformer块间插入自编码器式瓶颈会严重损害收敛（Bian et al., 2024）；最新方法Subspace Models (SM)声称可达100×压缩，但依赖每层投影矩阵共享低秩子空间约束、需在Grassmann流形上周期性更新子空间，并需修改AdamW（行级恒定学习率），偏离了纯端到端训练范式且工程复杂度高。此外，作者的谱分析发现AdamW会诱导输出投影矩阵列空间的秩坍缩（低秩结构），而Muon保持更高有效秩——这一差异直接影响可压缩空间的大小，为优化器选择提供了依据。
