Method: 1) 架构设计：将Transformer块的残差更新一般化为 x_{l+1} = x_l·P_l^id + F(x_l, W_l)，其中P_l^id为定义在恒等分支上的矩形恒等投影算子（I_{c×C}，c<C时补零列，c>C时截断列，c=C时退化为标准恒等阵），其复合性质 ∏ I_{c_i×c_{i+1}} = I_{c_0×c_D} 保证维度变化的通用性并保留秩k=min(c_i)的主坐标梯度通路。2) 瓶颈层：置于每个Transformer块（末块除外，共7个）的FFN之后，为自编码器结构，编码器E_l与解码器D_{l+1}均为"两层线性+非线性"，分置流水线边界两侧，仅传输b_l=E_l(z_l)∈R^{L×h}（h=4096/压缩率，100×→h=40，128×→h=32）；编码器并入第l层的残差函数，解码器并入第l+1层注意力前的本地残差分支，从而避免在恒等路径上直接施加压缩。3) 优化策略：基线用AdamW（作为公平对比目标，因其秩坍缩提供可压缩冗余），ResBM瓶颈模型用Muon（Newton-Schulz正交化梯度动量，通过避免瓶颈内秩坍缩最大化信息流），所有参数同频端到端更新。4) 实验设置：基线为Llama-3架构约2B参数（8个Transformer块、hidden 4096、qk-norm、vocab 50K、ctx 1024、C4、batch 32、峰值LR 2e-4、warmup 500步、余弦衰减），最终LR为峰值的1%（AdamW）/10%（Muon）；对比对象为自行复现的SM。5) 评测：26B tokens预训练困惑度对比（100×：21.60，128×：21.77，基线21.75）；吞吐测试采用8×A10G + GPipe式PyTorch分布式/NCCL，每块一GPU，对比10 Gbps中心化基线与80 Mbps去中心化基线及4种ResBM变体（AdamW/Muon × 100×/128×），batch降为16，训练12小时；另测带宽从80 Mbps到10 Gbps范围的吞吐增益曲线，并对瓶颈输出投影W_fc5逐层做SVD有效秩r_eff=Σσ_i²/max σ_i² 分析（Muon vs AdamW，token匹配~12.9B）。

Result: 1) 收敛性：ResBM在同等优化器下稳定优于SM；ResBM+Muon显著优于ResBM+AdamW（AdamW的秩坍缩会进一步压缩本已狭窄的瓶颈容量）。在26B tokens（C4，约占40B token计算最优预算的65%）上，100×与128×压缩的ResBM-Muon最终PPL分别为21.60与21.77，与未压缩AdamW基线21.75持平甚至略优；未压缩基线仅在训练极早期收敛更快，随后被追平/超越。2) 吞吐（8×A10G，80 Mbps）：中心化基线（10 Gbps）7530 TPS/loss 3.86；去中心化未压缩基线（80 Mbps）崩塌至609 TPS/loss 5.28；ResBM-AdamW 100×=7681 TPS、128×=7837 TPS；ResBM-Muon 100×与128×均为6795 TPS，loss分别为4.03与3.98——在慢125×的链路上恢复了近乎全部中心化吞吐。3) 带宽扫描：高带宽（800 Mbps–10 Gbps）增益约1×（通信非瓶颈）；低于该阈值后急剧上升，80 Mbps时AdamW≈12.8×、Muon≈11.2×。4) 开销：参数从1,896,751,104增至1,960,157,184（+63.4M，3.3%）；每边界传输从1×1024×4096（8 MiB bf16）降至1×1024×32（64 KiB），8阶段7边界每步总量112 MiB→896 KiB，前向激活与反向梯度均获益；Muon变体吞吐略低于AdamW源于其更高单步计算成本（与通信量无关）。5) 谱分析：基线上AdamW奇异值快速衰减（低秩），Muon保持更高有效秩且收敛更快（但更难压缩）；瓶颈层有效秩均值Muon 13.96 vs AdamW 13.22，差异主要在第1层（Muon 19.95 vs AdamW 10.26，接近2×差距），表明Muon在网络入口处前置分配容量（此处信息损失不可恢复），作者据此推测非均匀瓶颈（早层分配更多维度）可能进一步优化效率-质量权衡。

Conclusion: ResBM以"为通信效率而设计的架构"替代"为中心化架构加压缩补丁"的路线，通过在残差流旁路（而非之上）设置可学习低维子空间投影，并借助矩形恒等投影算子维持显式低秩恒等路径，实现了128×激活与激活梯度压缩且相对未压缩基线无收敛率退化，确立了新的SOTA。它与SM的关键差异在于：全部参数（瓶颈权重与主干）以同一频率端到端更新，无需Grassmann流形上的周期性子空间优化、无需修改AdamW，因而可直接嵌入标准训练栈与现成优化器。实验证明在互联网级链路上，ResBM使去中心化PP预训练在吞吐上接近中心化水平，表明去中心化流水线并行预训练不再被阶段间通信从根本上限制，架构级通信效率设计是超越数据中心网络的可行路径。作者将结论定位为初步性的：未来需在更大参数规模、更广架构族与下游任务上验证（初步实验提示可推广），并探索早层加宽的非均匀瓶颈设计、跨更多优化器的谱分析、以及更高带宽区间的收益曲线。

Abstract: Unlocking large-scale low-bandwidth decentralized training has the potential to utilize otherwise untapped compute resources. In centralized settings, large-scale multi-node training is primarily enabled by data and pipeline parallelism, two techniques that require ultra-high-bandwidth communication. While efficient methods now exist for decentralized data parallelism, pipeline parallelism remains the primary challenge. Recent efforts, such as Subspace Models (SM), have claimed up to 100x activation compression but rely on complex constrained optimization and diverge from true end-to-end training. In this paper, we propose a different approach, based on an architecture designed from the ground up to be native to low-bandwidth communication environments while still applicable to any standard transformer-based architecture. We call this architecture the Residual Bottleneck Model or ResBM, it introduces a residual encoder-decoder bottleneck module across pipeline boundaries that can be trained end-to-end as part of the model's parameters while preserving an explicit low-rank identity path. We show that ResBMs achieve state-of-the-art 128x activation compression without significant loss in convergence rates and without significant memory or compute overhead.

</details>


### [163] [Active Imitation Learning for Thermal- and Kernel-Aware LFM Inference on 3D S-NUCA Many-Cores](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11948)
*Yixian Shen,Chaoyao Shen,Jan Deen,George Floros,Andy Pimentel,Anuj Pathania*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对大模型(LFM)在3D堆叠S-NUCA架构CPU上的高效推理问题，提出基于主动模仿学习的AILFM调度框架，通过学习Oracle演示实现热感知调度，在保证热安全的同时最大化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPU资源有限且成本高昂，促使采用高性能3D堆叠S-NUCA架构CPU进行LFM推理。然而3D NoC带来的热问题和不均匀缓存延迟，以及LFM内核多样性和系统异构性，使得线程迁移和电压/频率缩放的优化管理变得极为困难，现有热管理方法依赖简化分析模型且缺乏适应性。

Method: 提出AILFM框架，基于主动模仿学习(AIL)从Oracle演示中学习近优的热感知调度策略，同时考虑核心级性能异构性和LFM内核特定行为。

Result: 实验表明AILFM优于现有基线方法，并能很好地泛化到不同的LFM工作负载。

Conclusion: AILFM能够有效平衡热安全与性能提升，为3D堆叠架构上的大模型推理提供了一种可行的智能调度方案。

Abstract: Large Foundation Model (LFM) inference is both memory- and compute-intensive, traditionally relying on GPUs. However, the limited availability and high cost have motivated the adoption of high-performance general-purpose CPUs, especially emerging 3D-stacked Static Non-Uniform Cache Architecture (3D S-NUCA) systems. These architectures offer enhanced bandwidth and locality but suffer from severe thermal challenges and uneven cache latencies due to 3D Networks-on-Chip (NoC). Optimal management of thread migration and V/f scaling is non-trivial due to LFM kernel diversity and system heterogeneity. Existing thermal management approaches often rely on oversimplified analytical models and lack adaptability. We propose AILFM, an Active Imitation Learning (AIL)-based scheduling framework that learns near-optimal thermal-aware scheduling policies from Oracle demonstrations with minimal run-time overhead. AILFM accounts for both core-level performance heterogeneity and kernel-specific behavior in LFMs to maintain thermal safety while maximizing performance. Extensive experiments show that AILFM outperforms state-of-the-art baselines and generalizes well across diverse LFM workloads.

</details>


### [164] [Decentralized Learning via Random Walk with Jumps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12260)
*Zonghong Liu,Matthew Dwyer,Salim El Rouayheb*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究无中心协调器的网络化去中心化学习中的"令牌式"随机游走学习（random-walk SGD）。作者发现：用 Metropolis–Hastings (MH) 算法在稀疏网络上实现加权采样时，随机游走会陷入所谓的"禁锢"（entrapment）现象——被长期困在少数高重要性节点所在的局部区域，导致更新高度相关、收敛严重退化。为解决该问题，作者提出 MHLJ（Metropolis–Hastings with Lévy jumps）：以小概率 p_J 触发由截断几何分布确定跳距的多次单跳（无更新）长程跳跃，打破细致平衡以恢复探索性，同时仅使用局部信息。理论给出包含数据异质性、网络谱隙 η 与跳跃概率 p_J 的收敛界：线性衰减项 + 谱隙控制的方差项 γL̄σ²_max/(ημ²) + 跳跃导致的稳态误差项 L̄³p_J²‖π−π̃‖²_TV σ²_max/(μ³L_min²)。实验表明 MHLJ 在环、2D 网格、Watts–Strogatz 等稀疏图上有效消除禁锢并显著加速收敛，通信开销增幅仅约 10%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 1. 去中心化学习的现实需求：数据天然分散在手机、传感器、边缘节点，集中式联邦学习存在通信瓶颈、隐私风险与单点故障；随机游走式学习每轮仅一条活跃链路、仅一个模型副本，计算与通信开销极低，适合带宽/能量受限网络。2. 加权采样的加速潜力：集中式 SGD 中按局部 Lipschitz 常数 L_i 加权采样可显著加速异质数据下的收敛（Needell et al.），去中心化场景中 Ayache et al. 也通过 MH 转移矩阵实现了相同的平稳分布 π_IS(v)=L_v/ΣL_u。3. 关键未解难题：作者发现，当数据异质性大（节点 L_v 差异显著）且网络连通性差（稀疏、环拓扑）时，MH 加权转移矩阵会让游走被"重要节点"吸附——跳出概率极小（如 5 节点环上约为 1/102），模型反复用同一批数据更新，被推向局部极小值，全局效果被边缘化，收敛显著变慢，这与先前文献报告的加速结论相悖。该禁锢现象是去中心化加权采样独有的、在集中式或全连接设置下不存在的缺陷。4. 因此需要一种既保持局部通信约束与低开销，又能打破局部禁锢、促进混合的转移机制（受 Lévy 跳跃/网络科学中长程跳跃启发）。

Method: 问题设置：图 G=(V,E)（每点含自环），局部损失 f_v 强凸（μ）且 Lipschitz 光滑（L_v），以 L_v 作为异质性代理（L_min ≈ L̄ ≤ L_max）；目标 min_x (1/|V|)Σ_v f_v(x)。随机游走 SGD：持有令牌的节点用本地梯度做 x^{t+1}=x^t − γ (L̄/L_{v_t}) ∇f_{v_t}(x^t) 更新，再按转移矩阵 P 传给邻居。三种基线转移设计：均匀邻居选择（度偏置）、MH 均匀平稳分布 P(v,u)=min{1/deg(v),1/deg(u)}、MH 加权采样 P_IS(v,u)=min{1/deg(v), L_u π_v/(deg(u) L_v)}（满足细致平衡，平稳分布为 π_IS）。

提出的 MHLJ：P_MHLJ = P_IS + p_J (P_Lévy − P_IS)，其中 P_Lévy = Σ_{i=1}^{r} [p_d(1−p_d)^{i−1}/(1−(1−p_d)^r)] · diag{A_G^i 1}^{−1} A_G^i（A_G 为邻接矩阵）。算法流程（Algorithm 1）：每步先做一次梯度更新；随后以 Bernoulli(p_J) 决定是否跳跃——若是，采样截断几何跳距 d~Geom(p_d, r)，再执行 d 次"均匀选邻居、无更新"的一跳传递（仅需当前节点邻域信息，符合去中心化约束）；否则按 P_IS 转移。跳跃以小概率发生，用以在保持目标平稳分布偏差可控的同时打破细致平衡、推出禁锢区域。

理论框架：沿用 Even et al. 的辅助序列技巧并加以推广，构造 y^t = y^{t−1} − γ w(v_{t−1})∇f_{v_{t−1}}(x*)，控制 ‖x^t−y^t‖²（Lemma 1，与随机性无关）；Lemma 2 用 Naor et al. 的鞅/谱隙不等式与 Markov 链遍历时间控制累积梯度偏差，得 E‖Σ w(v_i)∇f_{v_i}(x*)‖² ≤ (t−s)w_max σ_*²/η（可逆情形）。核心困难有二：一是单步条件偏差 E[∇f_{v_t}(x^t)|v_{t−1}] ≠ ∇f(x^t)（拓扑造成）；二是 MHLJ 的更新不再遍历无偏，E_{v~π}[w(v)∇f_v(x*)] ≠ 0（一阶最优条件不再适用），故需分离"依赖采样偏差"与"平稳分布偏差"两部分误差。

Result: 1. 禁锢现象的刻画与机制解释：5 节点环上若某节点 L_v≈100 而其余≈1，MH 转移给出留驻概率≈50/51、跳出概率≈1/102；在 Ring(1000) 上 MH 加权采样的收敛速率比均匀采样显著变慢，与已有文献结论相反；禁锢在环、2D 网格、Watts–Strogatz 等稀疏图上普遍存在，而 ER(1000,0.1) 等良连接图上不存在。机制为：细致平衡 + 大 L_v 差异 + 稀疏连通 ⇒ 重要节点近乎不可离开，梯度聚合严重偏置。

2. 主定理（Theorem 1，MHLJ 收敛率）：在假设 1–4、γ<1/L̄ 下，E‖x^T−x*‖² ≤ 2(1−γμ)^T‖x0−x*‖² + γL̄σ²_max/(ημ²) + L̄³p_J²‖π−π̃‖²_TV σ²_max/(μ³L_min²)。三项分别对应：线性衰减项、随机梯度方差项（由谱隙 η 控制，随 T 亚线性收敛并主导稳态误差）、跳跃引起的平稳分布偏离误差项（与步长无关，可由小 p_J 控制，但 p_J 过小又会加剧禁锢、降低谱隙）。Corollary 1 给出 ε-精度迭代复杂度 T = ⌈log(4‖x0−x*‖²/ε)·(L̄²σ_*²/(εη L_min μ³))⌉ 及残余误差地板 L̄³p_J²‖π−π̃‖²_TV σ²_max/(L_min²μ⁴)。

3. 重新审视随机游走 SGD（Theorem 2 / Corollary 2，无跳跃特例）：E‖x^T−x*‖² ≤ 2(1−γμ)^T‖x0−x*‖² + γL̄²σ_*²/(L_min η_IS μ²)；迭代复杂度与集中式加权采样（Needell et al.）同阶，差别仅在常数相乘与额外的谱隙因子 η_IS。同时指出加权采样允许步长 γ≤1/L̄（而非 γ≤1/L_max），因 L_max 大时稀疏图谱隙可能极小、误差项更易恶化；并指出"遍历无偏"在非插值情形下不仅充分而且必要——若遍历期望梯度偏离真梯度，算法会收敛到错误点，且该偏离可被量化。

4. 计算/通信开销：跳跃为多次无更新传递，每次更新期望跳转次数 ≤ (1−p_J)·1 + p_J E[d] ≤ 1 + p_J(1/p_d − 1)；示例中 p_J=0.1 时上界 1.1，即平均通信成本增加不超过 10%，而计算量（更新次数）明显减少。

5. 实验（线性回归，异质数据 σ_L²=1 概率 0.998、σ_H²=100 概率 0.002，节点数 1000）：ER(1000,0.1) 上同质数据时 UnifRW/WeightRW/MHLJ 表现相近，异质数据时 WeightRW 优于 UnifRW 且 MHLJ 更快；环、2D 网格、Watts–Strogatz(1000,4,0.1) 上均观察到禁锢，MHLJ 显著加速收敛；对比混合分布 MixedRW（π_λ=λ/|V|+(1−λ)L_v/ΣL_v）——混合分布也能提速但仍慢于 MHLJ，且最优 λ 依赖数据与图结构、η_λ 关于 π_λ 非凸，工程上难以整定。

6. 残余误差的两种工程消除机制：(i) 充分混合后切换到均匀（MH）采样——可完全消除误差地板且不牺牲速度，切换时机可通过保存近期梯度历史、以梯度和接近 0 为判据自动判定；(ii) 逐步降低跳跃概率 p_J——实验未能回收误差地板，原因在于当 σ_*² 很小时误差地板本身很小，而第一项中的谱隙项已占主导。

Conclusion: 论文的核心贡献是识别并形式化了"随机游走禁锢"这一此前未被研究的现象：把集中式加权采样搬移到稀疏去中心化网络并用 MH 转移实现时，会因细致平衡与重要性差异而将游走长期锁定在局部高重要性区域，使更新高度相关、收敛退化，这是去中心化加权采样的固有局限。所提出的 MHLJ 通过以小概率 p_J 施加 Lévy 型长程（多次单跳、无更新）跳跃，在仅使用局部邻居信息的前提下打破细致平衡并恢复探索性，提供"spectral gap 控制的混合速度"与"p_J 控制的采样偏差"之间的可调权衡。理论上建立了含扰动项的随机游走 SGD 收敛分析框架（辅助序列 + 遍历偏差量化），既给出与集中式加权采样可比的快速收敛界（额外仅乘谱隙因子），也明确了跳跃引入的、与步长无关的稳态误差地板并给出可量化的偏差度量 ‖π−π̃‖_TV。实验在多种稀疏图族上验证了 MHLJ 消除禁锢并加速收敛，且通信开销增加可控（≤10%）；残余误差可通过"混合后切换均匀采样"有效消除。局限与展望方面：跳跃带来通信与驻留时间开销、p_J 与 p_d 需按图结构调参、误差地板依赖于 σ_*² 与 L_min 等数据量纲，混合分布基线显示部分加速也可通过调 λ 获得但整定困难；进一步方向包括更精细的跳跃设计（如按节点局部性/重要性自适应的跳距分布）、非强凸/非光滑推广、以及在真实设备网络上的部署验证。

Abstract: We study decentralized learning over networks where data are distributed across nodes without a central coordinator. Random walk learning is a token-based approach in which a single model is propagated across the network and updated at each visited node using local data, thereby incurring low communication and computational overheads. In weighted random-walk learning, the transition matrix is designed to achieve a desired sampling distribution, thereby speeding up convergence under data heterogeneity. We show that implementing weighted sampling via the Metropolis-Hastings algorithm can lead to a previously unexplored phenomenon we term entrapment. The random walk may become trapped in a small region of the network, resulting in highly correlated updates and severely degraded convergence. To address this issue, we propose Metropolis-Hastings with Levy jumps, which introduces occasional long-range transitions to restore exploration while respecting local information constraints. We establish a convergence rate that explicitly characterizes the roles of data heterogeneity, network spectral gap, and jump probability, and demonstrate through experiments that MHLJ effectively eliminates entrapment and significantly speeds up decentralized learning.

</details>


### [165] [The Linear Centroids Hypothesis: How Deep Network Features Represent Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11962)
*Thomas Walker,Ahmed Imtiaz Humayun,Randall Balestriero,Richard Baraniuk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"线性质心假设"（Linear Centroids Hypothesis, LCH），主张深度网络（DN）所提取的特征对应于其输入空间局部区域内质心（centroid）的线性方向。相比线性表示假设（LRH），LCH 将分析单位从激活转向质心这一"功能行为的向量摘要"，既保留线性可解释性工具（如稀疏自编码器 SAE）的适用性，又能跨子组件（多层）分析、可识别回路（circuits），并且对 DINO 视觉 Transformer 能得到更稀疏且下游任务表现更好的特征字典。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可解释性研究的核心问题之一是深度网络究竟从输入中提取了哪些特征。线性表示假设（LRH）将特征定义为网络中隐空间激活的线性方向，是当前主流框架（配合稀疏自编码器 SAE 等工具）。但作者指出 LRH 存在三方面局限：(1) 它抽象掉了具体组件（神经元、层），难以定位到具体部件；(2) 容易识别出虚假/伪相关（spurious）特征；(3) 无法跨子组件（如多层之间）统一应用。动机由此产生：需要一个既保留线性方向这一可操作性、又锚定到具体网络组件与功能行为的新特征定义框架，并希望它能自然地复用现有 LRH 生态工具。

Method: 核心定义：LCH 认为特征对应于 DN 在其输入空间某个局部区域内行为的"质心向量"（centroid，是对该局部区域输入分布下模型功能/输出行为的向量化摘要）所张成的线性方向。具体实现路径为：计算网络各层（或各子组件）局部输入区域对应的质心；将现有 LRH 分析工具（如稀疏自编码器）直接作用于这些质心向量而非原始隐层激活，从而得到特征字典。实验方面：(1) 在 DINO 视觉 Transformer 上，将 SAE 应用于质心，与应用于激活的基线对比特征字典的稀疏性；(2) 将得到的特征用于下游任务评测；(3) 探索 LCH 支持的新型可解释性任务——在 GPT2-Large 中识别并追踪跨层的电路（circuits）。代码开源于 GitHub（ThomasWalker1/LinearCentroidsHypothesis）。

Result: 将 SAE 等 LRH 工具迁移到质心上，可为 DINO 视觉 Transformer 产生比在原始激活上更稀疏的特征字典，同时这些特征在下游任务上的表现更好，表明质心所张成的方向更能捕捉有意义的、可泛化的特征而非虚假特征。LCH 还支持新的分析范式：可较自然地在 GPT2-Large 中识别出跨层的功能电路，说明基于质心的方向在跨子组件（多层）分析上比 LRH 更有优势。

Conclusion: LCH 作为一个新的特征识别框架，把"特征"重新锚定在网络功能行为的向量化摘要（质心）之上，补足了 LRH 在组件粒度、虚假特征与跨子组件分析上的缺陷，并可无缝复用稀疏自编码器等成熟工具，在视觉与语言模型上均展现出有效性（更稀疏的字典、更优的下游表现、可识别电路）。未来方向包括：将 LCH 与神经元级/层级归因分析结合，进一步提升定位精度与因果验证能力。

Abstract: Identifying and understanding the features that a deep network (DN) extracts from its inputs to produce its outputs is a focal point of interpretability research. The Linear Representation Hypothesis (LRH) identifies features in terms of the linear directions formed by the inputs in a DN's latent space. However, the LRH is limited as it abstracts away from individual components (e.g., neurons and layers), is susceptible to identifying spurious features, and cannot be applied across sub-components (e.g., multiple layers). In this paper, we introduce the Linear Centroids Hypothesis (LCH) as a new framework for identifying the features of a DN. The LCH posits that features correspond to linear directions of centroids, which are vector summarizations of the functional behavior of a DN in a local region of its input space. Interpretability studies under the LCH can leverage existing LRH tools, such as sparse autoencoders, by applying them to the DN's centroids rather than to its latent activations. We demonstrate that doing so yields sparser feature dictionaries for DINO vision transformers, which also perform better on downstream tasks. The LCH also inspires novel approaches to interpretability; for example, LCH can readily identify circuits in GPT2-Large. For code to study the LCH https://github.com/ThomasWalker1/LinearCentroidsHypothesis .

</details>


### [166] [Classification of Epileptic iEEG using Topological Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11971)
*Sunia Tanweer,Narayan Puthanmadam Subramaniyam,Firas A. Khasawneh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究利用拓扑数据分析(TDA)方法从55例癫痫患者的颅内脑电图(iEEG)信号中检测癫痫发作。通过对持久图进行向量化和降维处理，三分类(发作前期、发作期、发作间期)达到80%的平衡准确率。研究发现，经典机器学习模型与深度学习模型表现相当，表明TDA特征能有效降低模型复杂度，同时强调了对多通道神经数据应用拓扑表示时保持结构降维的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 癫痫发作检测面临重大挑战：脑电信号具有高维度、非线性及潜在随机动力学特征。传统方法难以有效捕捉这些复杂特性，需要探索新的特征提取方法以提高分类性能。

Method: 研究使用55例癫痫患者的多通道iEEG数据，通过拓扑数据分析(TDA)提取特征。具体方法包括：1)从iEEG信号构建持久图；2)使用Carlsson坐标、持久图像和模板函数进行向量化和特征表示；3)开展大规模消融实验，涵盖多个频段、多种降维技术和分类器架构进行对比分析。

Result: 降维后的拓扑表示在三分类任务中达到80%的平衡准确率。值得注意的是，经典机器学习模型(79.17%)与深度学习模型性能相当。然而，保留完整多通道特征结构的pipeline因高维特征空间而出现严重过拟合。

Conclusion: 精心设计的拓扑特征可显著降低模型复杂度需求，经典ML模型在此任务中具有实用价值。结构保持的降维处理对于将拓扑表示应用于多通道神经数据至关重要，这对未来神经信号分析具有重要指导意义。

Abstract: Epileptic seizure detection from EEG signals remains challenging due to the high dimensionality and nonlinear, potentially stochastic, dynamics of neural activity. In this work, we investigate whether features derived from topological data analysis (TDA) can improve the classification of brain states in preictal, ictal and interictal iEEG recordings from epilepsy patients using multichannel data. We analyze data from 55 patients, significantly larger than many previous studies that rely on patient-specific models. Persistence diagrams derived from iEEG signals are vectorized using several TDA representations, including Carlsson coordinates, persistence images, and template functions. To understand how topological representations interact with modern machine learning pipelines, we conduct a large-scale ablation study across multiple iEEG frequency bands, dimensionality reduction techniques, feature representations, and classifier architectures. Our experiments show that dimension-reduced topological representations achieve up to 80\% balanced accuracy for three-class classification. Interestingly, classical machine learning models perform comparably to deep learning models, achieving up to 79.17\% balanced accuracy, suggesting that carefully designed topological features can substantially reduce model complexity requirements. In contrast, pipelines preserving the full multichannel feature structure exhibit severe overfitting due to the high-dimensional feature space. These findings highlight the importance of structure-preserving dimensionality reduction when applying topology-based representations to multichannel neural data.

</details>


### [167] [Multi-Head Residual-Gated DeepONet for Coherent Nonlinear Wave Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11972)
*Zhiwei Fan,Yiming Pan,Daniel Coca*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MH-RG DeepONet框架，通过多头部残差门控机制将紧凑物理描述符作为调制因子融入深度算子学习，以更高效、更物理一致的方式处理相干非线性波动力学建模问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统神经算子在处理波动力学问题时，将输入-输出映射视为黑箱高维回归问题，未能显式利用初始状态的紧凑物理描述符这一关键结构化物理上下文。现有特征融合策略（如直接拼接或FiLM仿射调制）存在参数爆炸或表达能力不足的问题。

Method: 提出Multi-Head Residual-Gated DeepONet (MH-RG)，包含三条平行路径：① 标准DeepONet状态路径（branch-trunk结构）；② 物理描述符 Conditioning 路径；③ 低秩多头机制用于捕获多个互补的条件响应模式。核心创新包括预分支残差调制器、分支残差门和躯干残差门的设计，实现物理描述符作为残差调制因子对状态预测的调节。

Result: 在保守波动力学和耗散束缚动力学两类高度非线性基准问题上，MH-RG DeepONet相比特征增强基线方法一致获得更低误差，且更好地保持了相位一致性和物理相关动力学量的保真度。

Conclusion: 通过将物理描述符显式嵌入为残差调制因子而非简单特征拼接，MH-RG DeepONet能够以更少的参数和更物理一致的方式捕捉波场的条件响应，为相干非线性波动力学的神经算子建模提供了更优范式。

Abstract: Coherent nonlinear wave dynamics are often strongly shaped by a compact set of physically meaningful descriptors of the initial state. Traditional neural operators typically treat the input-output mapping as a largely black-box high-dimensional regression problem, without explicitly exploiting this structured physical context. Common feature-integration strategies usually rely on direct concatenation or FiLM-style affine modulation in hidden latent spaces. Here we introduce a different paradigm, loosely inspired by the complementary roles of state evolution and physically meaningful observables in quantum mechanics: the wave field is learned through a standard DeepONet state pathway, while compact physical descriptors follow a parallel conditioning pathway and act as residual modulation factors on the state prediction. Based on this idea, we develop a Multi-Head Residual-Gated DeepONet (MH-RG), which combines a pre-branch residual modulator, a branch residual gate, and a trunk residual gate with a low-rank multi-head mechanism to capture multiple complementary conditioned response patterns without prohibitive parameter growth. We evaluate the framework on representative benchmarks including highly nonlinear conservative wave dynamics and dissipative trapped dynamics and further perform detailed mechanistic analyses of the learned multi-head gating behavior. Compared with feature-augmented baselines, MH-RG DeepONet achieves consistently lower error while better preserving phase coherence and the fidelity of physically relevant dynamical quantities.

</details>


### [168] [Exploring Concept Subspace for Self-explainable Text-Attributed Graph Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11986)
*Xiaoxue Han,Libo Zhang,Zining Zhu,Yue Ning*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Graph Concept Bottleneck (GCB) 提出了一种新的自解释图学习方法，将图映射到概念瓶颈空间进行可解释预测，并通过信息瓶颈原则优化概念选择，提升鲁棒性和泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可解释图学习方法主要依赖子图作为解释，缺乏新的解释形式；需要一种既能保证预测准确性又具有内在可解释性的方法。

Method: GCB将图映射到概念瓶颈子空间（每个概念为有意义的短语），基于概念激活进行预测；应用信息瓶颈原则筛选最相关概念，形成更简洁、忠实于模型的解释。

Result: GCB在保持与黑盒GNN相当精度的同时实现了内在可解释性；在分布偏移和数据扰动下表现更优，展现出更好的鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: 概念引导的预测范式可有效提升模型鲁棒性和泛化能力，概念瓶颈为图学习提供了一种新的可解释形式。

Abstract: We introduce Graph Concept Bottleneck (GCB) as a new paradigm for self-explainable text-attributed graph learning. GCB maps graphs into a subspace, concept bottleneck, where each concept is a meaningful phrase, and predictions are made based on the activation of these concepts. Unlike existing interpretable graph learning methods that primarily rely on subgraphs as explanations, the concept bottleneck provides a new form of interpretation. To refine the concept space, we apply the information bottleneck principle to focus on the most relevant concepts. This not only yields more concise and faithful explanations but also explicitly guides the model to "think" toward the correct decision. We empirically show that GCB achieves intrinsic interpretability with accuracy on par with black-box Graph Neural Networks. Moreover, it delivers better performance under distribution shifts and data perturbations, showing improved robustness and generalizability, benefitting from concept-guided prediction.

</details>


### [169] [LLM-Enhanced Log Anomaly Detection: A Comprehensive Benchmark of Large Language Models for Automated System Diagnostics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12218)
*Disha Patel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究是一个系统性基准测试，对比了传统方法与LLM方法在系统日志异常检测上的表现。研究涵盖三种方法类别、四个公开数据集，结果表明微调Transformer达到最高F1分数(0.96-0.99)，但提示型LLM在零样本情况下也能达到F1 0.82-0.91，且无需标注数据，为实际部署提供了灵活性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系统日志异常检测对大规模软件系统可靠性至关重要，但传统方法难以应对现代日志数据的异构性和动态演化特征。尽管大语言模型(LLM)在日志理解方面展现出潜力，但目前缺乏LLM方法与经典技术的系统性对比研究。

Method: 研究采用综合基准测试方法，评估三个类别的检测方法：1) 经典日志解析器(Drain、Spell、AEL)配合机器学习分类器；2) 微调Transformer模型(BERT、RoBERTa)；3) 提示型LLM(GPT-3.5、GPT-4、LLaMA-3)的零样本和少样本设置。实验在四个公开数据集(HDFS、BGL、Thunderbird、Spirit)上进行。

Result: 微调Transformer模型取得最高F1分数(0.96-0.99)；提示型LLM展现出显著的零样本能力(F1: 0.82-0.91)，无需任何标注训练数据。研究还分析了各方法的成本-精度权衡、延迟特性和失败模式。

Conclusion: 研究为实践者提供了可操作的指导方针，可根据精度、延迟、成本和标注数据可用性等具体约束条件选择合适的日志异常检测方法。所有代码和实验配置已公开以确保可复现性。

Abstract: System log anomaly detection is critical for maintaining the reliability of large-scale software systems, yet traditional methods struggle with the heterogeneous and evolving nature of modern log data. Recent advances in Large Language Models (LLMs) offer promising new approaches to log understanding, but a systematic comparison of LLM-based methods against established techniques remains lacking. In this paper, we present a comprehensive benchmark study evaluating both LLM-based and traditional approaches for log anomaly detection across four widely-used public datasets: HDFS, BGL, Thunderbird, and Spirit. We evaluate three categories of methods: (1) classical log parsers (Drain, Spell, AEL) combined with machine learning classifiers, (2) fine-tuned transformer models (BERT, RoBERTa), and (3) prompt-based LLM approaches (GPT-3.5, GPT-4, LLaMA-3) in zero-shot and few-shot settings. Our experiments reveal that while fine-tuned transformers achieve the highest F1-scores (0.96-0.99), prompt-based LLMs demonstrate remarkablezero-shot capabilities (F1: 0.82-0.91) without requiring any labeled training data -- a significant advantage for real-world deployment where labeled anomalies are scarce. We further analyze the cost-accuracy trade-offs, latency characteristics, and failure modes of each approach. Our findings provide actionable guidelines for practitioners choosing log anomaly detection methods based on their specific constraints regarding accuracy, latency, cost, and label availability. All code and experimental configurations are publicly available to facilitate reproducibility.

</details>


### [170] [Offline-Online Reinforcement Learning for Linear Mixture MDPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11994)
*Zhongjun Zhang,Sean R. Sinclair*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究线性混合MDP中离线-在线强化学习在环境偏移下的学习问题。提出一种自适应利用离线数据的算法，当离线数据信息丰富时可改进纯在线学习，当数据无信息时能安全忽略并保持在线学习性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线数据由未知行为策略收集，可能来自不匹配的环境，而在线阶段学习者需与目标环境交互。核心挑战是如何在环境偏移情况下有效利用离线数据提升学习效率，同时避免被低质量数据误导。

Method: 提出一种自适应算法，根据离线数据的信息量动态调整其使用程度。算法通过理论分析确定离线数据何时有益（覆盖充分或环境偏移较小），并据此决定是否利用离线数据。

Result: 建立了后悔度上界，明确刻画离线数据何时有益；证明了几乎匹配的下界。数值实验验证了理论结果。当离线数据具有足够覆盖或环境偏移较小时，算法优于纯在线学习；当离线数据无信息时，算法可安全忽略并达到在线学习性能。

Conclusion: 该工作为环境偏移下的离线-在线强化学习提供了原则性框架，理论保证严谨，能自适应平衡离线数据价值与风险。

Abstract: We study offline-online reinforcement learning in linear mixture Markov decision processes (MDPs) under environment shift. In the offline phase, data are collected by an unknown behavior policy and may come from a mismatched environment, while in the online phase the learner interacts with the target environment. We propose an algorithm that adaptively leverages offline data. When the offline data are informative, either due to sufficient coverage or small environment shift, the algorithm provably improves over purely online learning. When the offline data are uninformative, it safely ignores them and matches the online-only performance. We establish regret upper bounds that explicitly characterize when offline data are beneficial, together with nearly matching lower bounds. Numerical experiments further corroborate our theoretical findings.

</details>


### [171] [Loss-Driven Bayesian Active Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11995)
*Zhuoyue Huang,Freddie Bickford Smith,Tom Rainforth*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于损失的贝叶斯主动学习方法，使数据采集能够直接针对给定决策问题相关的损失函数。通过证明任何损失都可以推导出唯一的最优数据采集目标，并进一步表明任何取加权Bregman散度形式的损失都可以解析计算其对应目标的核心分量，从而实现该方法的实际应用。在多种损失的回归和分类实验中，该方法相比现有技术降低了测试损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主动学习方法在定制数据采集以适应不同下游问题和损失函数方面存在局限性，无法灵活地针对特定决策问题进行优化。

Method: 提出一种严格的损失驱动贝叶斯主动学习方法：1) 证明任何损失函数都可推导出唯一的数据采集目标；2) 证明取加权Bregman散度形式的损失允许解析计算目标的核心分量，使方法具备实用性。

Result: 在多种不同损失的回归和分类实验中，该方法相对于现有技术降低了测试损失。

Conclusion: 该损失驱动的主动学习方法具有良好的通用性，能够灵活适应不同的下游问题和损失函数，且具有理论支撑和实证验证。

Abstract: The central goal of active learning is to gather data that maximises downstream predictive performance, but popular approaches have limited flexibility in customising this data acquisition to different downstream problems and losses. We propose a rigorous loss-driven approach to Bayesian active learning that allows data acquisition to directly target the loss associated with a given decision problem. In particular, we show how any loss can be used to derive a unique objective for optimal data acquisition. Critically, we then show that any loss taking the form of a weighted Bregman divergence permits analytic computation of a central component of its corresponding objective, making the approach applicable in practice. In regression and classification experiments with a range of different losses, we find our approach reduces test losses relative to existing techniques.

</details>


### [172] [BayMOTH: Bayesian optiMizatiOn with meTa-lookahead -- a simple approacH](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12005)
*Rahman Ejaz,Varchas Gopalaswamy,Ricardo Luna,Aarne Lees,Vineet Gundecha,Christopher Kanan,Soumyendu Sarkar,Riccardo Betti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种简单的元贝叶斯优化(meta-BO)算法，核心思想是自适应地利用相关任务信息——当任务结构可迁移时使用元学习策略，否则回退到前向查找(lookahead)策略。实验表明该方法在任务相关性高时具有竞争力，同时在任务相关性低的场景下仍能保持良好性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 元贝叶斯优化(meta-BO)虽然能通过利用相关任务信息提升样本效率，但存在一个关键问题：当元训练任务与测试任务结构对齐不佳时，可能导致次优查询。文章指出，现有meta-BO方法缺乏对任务相关性强弱的自适应能力，在低相关场景下表现受限。

Method: 提出一种统一的框架，能够自适应判断是否利用相关任务信息：
1. 当任务结构可迁移时 → 利用元学习获得的先验知识
2. 当任务相关性低时 → 回退到lookahead策略（前向查找）
通过在统一框架内动态切换策略，实现对不同任务环境的鲁棒适应。

Result: 在函数优化任务上的实验表明：
- 在高任务相关性场景下，该方法与现有方法具有竞争力
- 在低任务相关性场景（测试任务与元训练集共享有限结构）下，仍能保持强劲性能
实现了"可迁移时利用，不可迁移时保守"的平衡策略。

Conclusion: 该工作成功解决了meta-BO中任务对齐敏感的核心问题，通过自适应机制在不同任务相关性条件下均表现出良好的鲁棒性和有效性，为提升元学习的实用性提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Bayesian optimization (BO) has for sequential optimization of expensive black-box functions demonstrated practicality and effectiveness in many real-world settings. Meta-Bayesian optimization (meta-BO) focuses on improving the sample efficiency of BO by making use of information from related tasks. Although meta-BO is sample-efficient when task structure transfers, poor alignment between meta-training and test tasks can cause suboptimal queries to be suggested during online optimization. To this end, we propose a simple meta-BO algorithm that utilizes related-task information when determined useful, falling back to lookahead otherwise, within a unified framework. We demonstrate competitiveness of our method with existing approaches on function optimization tasks, while retaining strong performance in low task-relatedness regimes where test tasks share limited structure with the meta-training set.

</details>


### [173] [Sample Complexity of Autoregressive Reasoning: Chain-of-Thought vs. End-to-End](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12013)
*Steve Hanneke,Idan Mehalel,Shay Moran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了自回归语言模型的PAC学习框架，分析了样本复杂度如何随生成长度T变化的问题。在端到端监督下，样本复杂度可呈现任意介于常数和线性之间的增长速率；而链式思维监督下，样本复杂度与T无关，证明中间推理步骤可完全消除生成长度的依赖性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归模型是现代大语言模型的核心，但其可学习性尚未得到充分理论分析。Joshi等人提出的PAC学习框架中，学习任务是理解由自回归过程诱导的输入-输出映射。两个核心问题是：样本复杂度如何随生成长度T变化？链式思维监督能在多大程度上减少这种依赖？

Method: 1. 建立自回归生成过程的数学模型，将T步迭代应用视为一个复合函数；2. 端到端学习场景下，通过构造反例展示样本复杂度增长速率的丰富景观；3. 链式思维监督场景下，证明样本复杂度与T的独立性；4. 引入新的组合数学工具进行分析。

Result: 1. 端到端学习：在温和条件下，样本复杂度的增长速率r(T)可以是任意介于常数和线性之间的函数，这给出了近乎完整的刻画；2. 链式思维监督：样本复杂度与T完全无关，表明中间推理步骤可消除生成长度依赖；3. 解决了Joshi等人提出的多个开放问题。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了链式思维监督在理论上的巨大优势——可完全消除生成长度对样本复杂度的影响。这为理解Chain-of-Thought推理在大模型中的有效性提供了理论依据，同时丰富了自回归模型可学习性的理论图景。

Abstract: Modern large language models generate text autoregressively, producing tokens one at a time. To study the learnability of such systems, Joshi et al. (COLT 2025) introduced a PAC-learning framework for next-token generators, the primitive underlying autoregressive models. In this framework, an unknown next-token generator maps a sequence of tokens to the next token and is iteratively applied for $T$ steps, producing a chain of tokens whose final token constitutes the model's output. The learning task is to learn the input-output mapping induced by this autoregressive process. Depending on the available supervision, training examples may reveal only the final output (End-to-End supervision) or the entire generated chain (Chain-of-Thought supervision). This raises two natural questions: how the sample complexity depends on the generation length $T$, and how much Chain-of-Thought supervision can reduce this dependence.
  In this work we give a nearly complete answer to both questions by uncovering a taxonomy of how the sample complexity scales with $T$. For End-to-End learning, we show that the landscape is remarkably rich: subject to mild conditions, essentially any growth rate $r(T)$ between constant and linear can arise as the sample complexity, and combined with the linear upper bound of Joshi et al., this yields an essentially complete characterization. In contrast, under Chain-of-Thought supervision we show that the sample complexity is independent of $T$, demonstrating that access to intermediate reasoning steps can eliminate the dependence on the generation length altogether. Our analysis introduces new combinatorial tools, and as corollaries we resolve several open questions posed by Joshi et al. regarding the dependence of learnability on the generation length and the role of Chain-of-Thought supervision.

</details>


### [174] [UCS: Estimating Unseen Coverage for Improved In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12015)
*Jiayi Xin,Xiang Li,Evan Qiang,Weiqing He,Tianqi Shang,Weijie J. Su,Qi Long*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: In-context learning (ICL) performance depends critically on which demonstrations are placed in the prompt, yet most existing selectors prioritize heuristic notions of relevance or diversity and provide limited insight into the coverage of a demonstration set. We propose Unseen Coverage Selection (UKS), a training-free, subset-level coverage prior motivated by the principle that a good demonstration set should expose the model to latent cluster unrevealed by the currently selected subset. UCS operationalizes this idea by (1) inducing discrete latent clusters from model-consistent embeddings and (2) estimating the number of unrevealed clusters within a candidate subset via a Smoothed Good--Turing estimator from its empirical frequency spectrum. Unlike previous selection methods, UCS is coverage-based and training-free, and can be seamlessly combined with both query-dependent and query-independent selection baselines via a simple regularized objective. Experiments on multiple intent-classification and reasoning benchmarks with frontier Large Language Models show that augmenting strong baselines with UCS consistently improves ICL accuracy by up to 2-6% under the same selection budget, while also yielding insights into task- and model-level latent cluster distributions. Code is available at https://github.com/Raina-Xin/UCS.

Conclusion: AI enhancement unavailable (ai_circuit_open). Original arXiv metadata is preserved.

Abstract: In-context learning (ICL) performance depends critically on which demonstrations are placed in the prompt, yet most existing selectors prioritize heuristic notions of relevance or diversity and provide limited insight into the coverage of a demonstration set. We propose Unseen Coverage Selection (UKS), a training-free, subset-level coverage prior motivated by the principle that a good demonstration set should expose the model to latent cluster unrevealed by the currently selected subset. UCS operationalizes this idea by (1) inducing discrete latent clusters from model-consistent embeddings and (2) estimating the number of unrevealed clusters within a candidate subset via a Smoothed Good--Turing estimator from its empirical frequency spectrum. Unlike previous selection methods, UCS is coverage-based and training-free, and can be seamlessly combined with both query-dependent and query-independent selection baselines via a simple regularized objective. Experiments on multiple intent-classification and reasoning benchmarks with frontier Large Language Models show that augmenting strong baselines with UCS consistently improves ICL accuracy by up to 2-6% under the same selection budget, while also yielding insights into task- and model-level latent cluster distributions. ### [175] [TriFit: Trimodal Fusion with Protein Dynamics for Mutation Fitness Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12026)
*Seungik Cho*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Predicting the functional impact of single amino acid substitutions (SAVs) is central to understanding genetic disease and engineering therapeutic proteins. While protein language models and structure-based methods have achieved strong performance on this task, they systematically neglect protein dynamics; residue flexibility, correlated motions, and allosteric coupling are well-established determinants of mutational tolerance in structural biology, yet have not been incorporated into supervised variant effect predictors. We present TriFit, a multimodal framework that integrates sequence, structure, and protein dynamics through a four-expert Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) fusion module with trimodal cross-modal contrastive learning. Sequence embeddings are extracted via masked marginal scoring with ESM-2 (650M); structural embeddings from AlphaFold2-predicted C-alpha geometries; and dynamics embeddings from Gaussian Network Model (GNM) B-factors, mode shapes, and residue-residue cross-correlations. The MoE router adaptively weights modality combinations conditioned on the input, enabling protein-specific fusion without fixed modality assumptions. On the ProteinGym substitution benchmark (217 DMS assays, 696k SAVs), TriFit achieves AUROC 0.897 +/- 0.0002, outperforming all supervised baselines including Kermut (0.864) and ProteinNPT (0.844), and the best zero-shot model ESM3 (0.769). Ablation studies confirm that dynamics provides the largest marginal contribution over pairwise modality combinations, and TriFit achieves well-calibrated probabilistic outputs (ECE = 0.044) without post-hoc correction.

Conclusion: AI enhancement unavailable (ai_circuit_open). Original arXiv metadata is preserved.

Ablation studies confirm that dynamics provides the largest marginal contribution over pairwise modality combinations, and TriFit achieves well-calibrated probabilistic outputs (ECE = 0.044) without post-hoc correction.

### [176] [VISTA: Validation-Informed Trajectory Adaptation via Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12044)
*Eli Corn,Daphna Weinshall*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Trajectory Deviation问题：深度学习模型在训练过程中可能放弃对特定数据子群体的高泛化状态，丢弃已学习的潜在特征，但不触发经典过拟合信号。VISTA框架通过识别早期模型状态中的"专家锚点"并进行覆盖加权集成，在线整合到训练过程中以正则化损失景观、保留已掌握的知识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习模型虽能获得强验证准确率，但仍可能收敛至次优解。训练过程中模型可能放弃对特定数据子群体的高泛化状态，丢弃已学习的潜在特征，且不触发经典过拟合信号。

Method: VISTA是一个在线自蒸馏框架，通过验证信息引导的边际覆盖分数（Marginal Coverage）识别"专家锚点"——即保留对不同数据区域专业化能力的早期模型状态。将这些锚点的覆盖加权集成在线整合到训练过程中，实现损失景观正则化并保留已掌握的知识。

Result: 在多个基准测试上评估，VISTA相比标准训练和先前自蒸馏方法展现出更强的鲁棒性和泛化能力。轻量级实现减少了90%存储开销且无性能损失。

Conclusion: VISTA通过在线自蒸馏与专家锚点机制有效解决了轨迹偏离问题，在提升泛化能力的同时显著降低了存储成本。

Abstract: Deep learning models may converge to suboptimal solutions despite strong validation accuracy, masking an optimization failure we term Trajectory Deviation. This is because as training proceeds, models can abandon high generalization states for specific data sub-populations, thus discarding previously learned latent features without triggering classical overfitting signals. To address this problem we introduce VISTA, an online self-distillation framework that enforces consistency along the optimization trajectory. Using a validation-informed Marginal Coverage score, VISTA identifies expert anchors, which are earlier model states that retain specialized competence over distinct data regions. A coverage-weighted ensemble of these anchors is integrated online during training, regularizing the loss landscape and preserving mastered knowledge. When evaluated across multiple benchmarks, VISTA demonstrates improved robustness and generalization over standard training and prior self-distillation methods, while a lightweight implementation reduces storage overhead by 90% without performance loss.

</details>


### [177] [Interpretable DNA Sequence Classification via Dynamic Feature Generation in Decision Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12060)
*Nicolas Huynh,Krzysztof Kacprzyk,Ryan Sheridan,David Bentley,Mihaela van der Schaar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: The analysis of DNA sequences has become critical in numerous fields, from evolutionary biology to understanding gene regulation and disease mechanisms. While deep neural networks can achieve remarkable predictive performance, they typically operate as black boxes. Contrasting these black boxes, axis-aligned decision trees offer a promising direction for interpretable DNA sequence analysis, yet they suffer from a fundamental limitation: considering individual raw features in isolation at each split limits their expressivity, which results in prohibitive tree depths that hinder both interpretability and generalization performance. We address this challenge by introducing DEFT, a novel framework that adaptively generates high-level sequence features during tree construction. DEFT leverages large language models to propose biologically-informed features tailored to the local sequence distributions at each node and to iteratively refine them with a reflection mechanism. Empirically, we demonstrate that DEFT discovers human-interpretable and highly predictive sequence features across a diverse range of genomic tasks.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: AI enhancement unavailable; see the original abstract.

Result: AI enhancement unavailable; see the original abstract.

Conclusion: AI enhancement unavailable (ai_circuit_open). Original arXiv metadata is preserved.

While deep neural networks can achieve remarkable predictive performance, they typically operate as black boxes. Contrasting these black boxes, axis-aligned decision trees offer a promising direction for interpretable DNA sequence analysis, yet they suffer from a fundamental limitation: considering individual raw features in isolation at each split limits their expressivity, which results in prohibitive tree depths that hinder both interpretability and generalization performance. We address this challenge by introducing DEFT, a novel framework that adaptively generates high-level sequence features during tree construction. DEFT leverages large language models to propose biologically-informed features tailored to the local sequence distributions at each node and to iteratively refine them with a reflection mechanism. Empirically, we demonstrate that DEFT discovers human-interpretable and highly predictive sequence features across a diverse range of genomic tasks.

</details>


### [178] [Robust Optimization for Mitigating Reward Hacking with Correlated Proxies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12086)
*Zixuan Liu,Xiaolin Sun,Zizhan Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Designing robust reinforcement learning (RL) agents in the presence of imperfect reward signals remains a core challenge. In practice, agents are often trained with proxy rewards that only approximate the true objective, leaving them vulnerable to reward hacking, where high proxy returns arise from unintended or exploitative behaviors. Recent work formalizes this issue using r-correlation between proxy and true rewards, but existing methods like occupancy-regularized policy optimization (ORPO) optimize against a fixed proxy and do not provide strong guarantees against broader classes of correlated proxies. In this work, we formulate reward hacking as a robust policy optimization problem over the space of all r-correlated proxy rewards. We derive a tractable max-min formulation, where the agent maximizes performance under the worst-case proxy consistent with the correlation constraint. We further show that when the reward is a linear function of known features, our approach can be adapted to incorporate this prior knowledge, yielding both improved policies and interpretable worst-case rewards. Experiments across several environments show that our algorithms consistently outperform ORPO in worst-case returns, and offer improved robustness and stability across different levels of proxy-true reward correlation. These results show that our approach provides both robustness and transparency in settings where reward design is inherently uncertain. The code is available at https://github.com/ZixuanLiu4869/reward_hacking.

Conclusion: AI enhancement unavailable (ai_circuit_open). Original arXiv metadata is preserved.

### [179] [SOLARIS: Speculative Offloading of Latent-bAsed Representation for Inference Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12110)
*Zikun Liu,Liang Luo,Qianru Li,Zhengyu Zhang,Wei Ling,Jingyi Shen,Zeliang Chen,Yaning Huang,Jingxian Huang,Abdallah Aboelela,Chonglin Sun,Feifan Gu,Fenggang Wu,Hang Qu,Huayu Li,Jill Pan,Kaidi Pei,Laming Chen,Longhao Jin,Qin Huang,Tongyi Tang,Varna Puvvada,Wenlin Chen,Xiaohan Wei,Xu Cao,Yantao Yao,Yuan Jin,Yunchen Pu,Yuxin Chen,Zijian Shen,Zhengkai Zhang,Dong Liang,Ellie Wen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SOLARIS是一种受推测解码启发的推荐系统框架，通过预测未来用户-物品交互对并异步预计算其嵌入表示，将昂贵的基座模型推理与延迟敏感的服务路径解耦。在Meta广告系统日均数十亿请求的规模下部署，实现了0.67%的主要收入指标提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型推荐基座模型虽性能优越但计算开销巨大，实时服务不切实际；现有知识蒸馏方案需牺牲质量换取效率，亟需在保持模型性能的同时实现高效推理。

Method: SOLARIS采用推测式预计算策略：1) 预测哪些用户-物品对可能在未来请求中出现；2) 异步预生成相应嵌入表示；3) 将基座模型推理从延迟关键路径中解耦，实现此前认为无法在线使用的高质量模型实时服务。

Result: Meta广告系统日均数十亿请求规模下部署，核心收入指标提升0.67%。

Conclusion: 证明了通过推测式解码框架可将此前认为推理成本过高的优质模型成功应用于实时服务，为大规模推荐系统的模型轻量化部署提供了新范式。

Abstract: Recent advances in recommendation scaling laws have led to foundation models of unprecedented complexity. While these models offer superior performance, their computational demands make real-time serving impractical, often forcing practitioners to rely on knowledge distillation-compromising serving quality for efficiency. To address this challenge, we present SOLARIS (Speculative Offloading of Latent-bAsed Representation for Inference Scaling), a novel framework inspired by speculative decoding. SOLARIS proactively precomputes user-item interaction embeddings by predicting which user-item pairs are likely to appear in future requests, and asynchronously generating their foundation model representations ahead of time. This approach decouples the costly foundation model inference from the latency-critical serving path, enabling real-time knowledge transfer from models previously considered too expensive for online use. Deployed across Meta's advertising system serving billions of daily requests, SOLARIS achieves 0.67% revenue-driving top-line metrics gain, demonstrating its effectiveness at scale.

</details>


### [180] [XANE(3): An E(3)-Equivariant Graph Neural Network for Accurate Prediction of XANES Spectra from Atomic Structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12140)
*Vitor F. Grizzi,Luke N. Pretzie,Jiayi Xu,Cong Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: We present XANE(3), a physics-based E(3)-equivariant graph neural network for predicting X-ray absorption near-edge structure (XANES) spectra directly from atomic structures. The model combines tensor-product message passing with spherical harmonic edge features, absorber-query attention pooling, custom equivariant layer normalization, adaptive gated residual connections, and a spectral readout based on a multi-scale Gaussian basis with an optional sigmoidal background term. To improve line-shape fidelity, training is performed with a composite objective that includes pointwise spectral reconstruction together with first- and second-derivative matching terms. We evaluate the model on a dataset of 5,941 FDMNES simulations of iron oxide surface facets and obtain a spectrum mean squared error of $1.0 \times 10^{-3}$ on the test set. The model accurately reproduces the main edge structure, relative peak intensities, pre-edge features, and post-edge oscillations. Ablation studies show that the derivative-aware objective, custom equivariant normalization, absorber-conditioned attention pooling, adaptive gated residual mixing, and global background term each improve performance. Interestingly, a capacity-matched scalar-only variant achieves comparable pointwise reconstruction error but reduced derivative-level fidelity, indicating that explicit tensorial channels are not strictly required for low intensity error on this dataset, although they remain beneficial for capturing finer spectral structure. These results establish XANE(3) as an accurate and efficient surrogate for XANES simulation and offer a promising route toward accelerated spectral prediction, ML-assisted spectroscopy, and data-driven materials discovery.

### [181] [Distinct mechanisms underlying in-context learning in transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12151)
*Cole Gibson,Wenping Cui,Gautam Reddy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Modern distributed networks, notably transformers, acquire a remarkable ability (termed `in-context learning') to adapt their computation to input statistics, such that a fixed network can be applied to data from a broad range of systems. Here, we provide a complete mechanistic characterization of this behavior in transformers trained on a finite set $S$ of discrete Markov chains. The transformer displays four algorithmic phases, characterized by whether the network memorizes and generalizes, and whether it uses 1-point or 2-point statistics. We show that the four phases are implemented by multi-layer subcircuits that exemplify two qualitatively distinct mechanisms for implementing context-adaptive computations. Minimal models isolate the key features of both motifs. Memorization and generalization phases are delineated by two boundaries that depend on data diversity, $K = |S|$. The first ($K_1^\ast$) is set by a kinetic competition between subcircuits and the second ($K_2^\ast$) is set by a representational bottleneck. A symmetry-constrained theory of a transformer's training dynamics explains the sharp transition from 1-point to 2-point generalization and identifies key features of the loss landscape that allow the network to generalize. Put together, we show that transformers develop distinct subcircuits to implement in-context learning and identify conditions that favor certain mechanisms over others.

Conclusion: AI enhancement unavailable (ai_circuit_open). Original arXiv metadata is preserved.

### [182] [PubSwap: Public-Data Off-Policy Coordination for Federated RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12160)
*Anupam Nayak,Baris Askin,Muhammed Ustaomeroglu,Carlee Joe-Wong,Gauri Joshi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种联邦强化学习框架，结合LoRA局部适配与公共数据离线策略步骤，解决去中心化场景下RLVR的可扩展性问题，在数学和医学推理基准上优于标准基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前RLVR研究集中于中心化设置，而现实应用涉及跨组织的去中心化私有数据。联邦训练面临全模型同步开销大、局部异质性导致严重客户端漂移两大挑战。

Method: 提出联邦RLVR框架：(1) 基于LoRA的局部适配保持通信高效；(2) 利用小型共享公共数据集进行周期性响应级训练信号交换，提供轻量级全局对齐锚点；(3) 在公共数据步骤中选择性替换本地错误响应为全局正确响应。

Result: 在多个数学和医学推理基准及模型上，方法一致优于标准基线。

Conclusion: 联邦推理后训练的有效方案：低秩通信+有限公共数据协调。该方法兼顾通信效率与跨客户端协调，同时保护私有数据隐私。

Abstract: Reasoning post-training with reinforcement learning from verifiable rewards (RLVR) is typically studied in centralized settings, yet many realistic applications involve decentralized private data distributed across organizations. Federated training is a natural solution, but scaling RLVR in this regime is challenging: full-model synchronization is expensive, and performing many local steps can cause severe client drift under heterogeneous data. We propose a federated RLVR framework that combines LoRA-based local adaptation with public-data-based off-policy steps to improve both communication efficiency and cross-client coordination. In particular, a small shared public dataset is used to periodically exchange and reuse response-level training signals across organizations, providing a lightweight anchor toward a more globally aligned objective without exposing private data. Our method selectively replaces locally incorrect responses with globally correct ones during public-data steps, thereby keeping training closer to the local policy while still benefiting from cross-client coordination. Across mathematical and medical reasoning benchmarks and models, our method consistently improves over standard baselines. Our results highlight a simple and effective recipe for federated reasoning post-training: combining low-rank communication with limited public-data coordination.

</details>


### [183] [CycloneMAE: A Scalable Multi-Task Learning Model for Global Tropical Cyclone Probabilistic Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12180)
*Renlong Hang,Zihao Xu,Jiuwei Zhao,Runling Yu,Leye Cheng,Qingshan Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CycloneMAE是一个基于Transformer的多任务预测模型，通过掩码自编码器学习热带气旋的可迁移表示，同时输出确定性预报和概率分布。在五个全球洋盆的评估中，该模型在120小时内的气压和风场预报以及24小时内的路径预报方面超越了主流数值天气预报系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有热带气旋预报面临核心矛盾：数值天气预报(NWP)计算成本高且难以利用历史数据，而现有深度学习模型缺乏跨变量泛化能力。本研究旨在构建一个可扩展的概率预报框架，同时解决计算效率和泛化性问题。

Method: 1) 采用TC结构感知掩码自编码器架构学习多模态数据的可迁移表示；2) 耦合离散概率网格化机制实现概率预报；3) 预训练/微调范式实现跨变量泛化。

Result: 在五个全球洋盆的评估中，CycloneMAE在120小时气压/风场预报和24小时路径预报上优于主流NWP系统。归因分析表明：短期预报主要依赖卫星影像中的内核对流结构，长期预报则逐步转向外部环境因素。

Conclusion: 该框架为热带气旋业务预报提供了一条可扩展、概率化、可解释的技术路径。

Abstract: Tropical cyclones (TCs) rank among the most destructive natural hazards, yet their forecasting faces fundamental trade-offs: numerical weather prediction (NWP) models are computationally prohibitive and struggle to leverage historical data, while existing deep learning (DL)-based intelligent models are variable-specific and deterministic, which fail to generalize across different forecasting variables. Here we present CycloneMAE, a scalable multi-task forecasting model that learns transferable TC representations from multi-modal data using a TC structure-aware masked autoencoder. By coupling a discrete probabilistic gridding mechanism with a pre-train/fine-tune paradigm, CycloneMAE simultaneously delivers deterministic forecasts and probability distributions. Evaluated across five global ocean basins, CycloneMAE outperforms leading NWP systems in pressure and wind forecasting up to 120 hours and in track forecasting up to 24 hours. Attribution analysis via integrated gradients reveals physically interpretable learning dynamics: short-term forecasts rely predominantly on the internal core convective structure from satellite imagery, whereas longer-term forecasts progressively shift attention to external environmental factors. Our framework establishes a scalable, probabilistic, and interpretable pathway for operational TC forecasting.

</details>


### [184] [Clustering-Enhanced Domain Adaptation for Cross-Domain Intrusion Detection in Industrial Control Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12183)
*Luyao Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对工业控制系统在动态环境下面临的跨域入侵检测挑战，本文提出一种聚类增强的域适应方法。该方法通过基于谱变换的特征对齐和K-Medoids聚类结合PCA降维，显著提升了对未知攻击的检测能力，在检测准确率上最高提升49%，聚类增强策略额外提升26%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业控制系统运行在动态环境中，流量分布随场景变化，标注样本有限，未知攻击频繁出现，传统方法难以有效应对跨域入侵检测问题。

Method: 提出聚类增强的域适应方法，包含两个核心组件：1）基于谱变换的迁移学习模块，将源域和目标域投影到共享潜在子空间，通过迭代减少分布差异；2）聚类增强策略，结合K-Medoids聚类与PCA降维，提升跨域相关性估计并减少人工参数调优带来的性能下降。

Result: 实验表明：相比五种基线模型，检测准确率最高提升49%，F-score提升显著，稳定性更强；聚类增强策略在代表性任务上额外提升检测准确率最高26%。

Conclusion: 该方法有效缓解了数据稀缺和域偏移问题，为动态工业环境下的鲁棒跨域入侵检测提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Industrial control systems operate in dynamic environments where traffic distributions vary across scenarios, labeled samples are limited, and unknown attacks frequently emerge, posing significant challenges to cross-domain intrusion detection. To address this issue, this paper proposes a clustering-enhanced domain adaptation method for industrial control traffic. The framework contains two key components. First, a feature-based transfer learning module projects source and target domains into a shared latent subspace through spectral-transform-based feature alignment and iteratively reduces distribution discrepancies, enabling accurate cross-domain detection. Second, a clustering enhancement strategy combines K-Medoids clustering with PCA-based dimensionality reduction to improve cross-domain correlation estimation and reduce performance degradation caused by manual parameter tuning. Experimental results show that the proposed method significantly improves unknown attack detection. Compared with five baseline models, it increases detection accuracy by up to 49%, achieves larger gains in F-score, and demonstrates stronger stability. Moreover, the clustering enhancement strategy further boosts detection accuracy by up to 26% on representative tasks. These results suggest that the proposed method effectively alleviates data scarcity and domain shift, providing a practical solution for robust cross-domain intrusion detection in dynamic industrial environments.

</details>


### [185] [MolMem: Memory-Augmented Agentic Reinforcement Learning for Sample-Efficient Molecular Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12237)
*Ziqing Wang,Yibo Wen,Abhishek Pandy,Han Liu,Kaize Ding*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MolMem是一种多轮智能体强化学习框架，通过双记忆系统（静态范例记忆+进化技能记忆）解决药物分子优化中样本效率低的问题，在仅500次oracle调用的预算下实现了单目标任务90%和多目标任务52%的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 药物发现中的分子优化面临关键挑战：每次oracle评估成本高昂，现有方法样本效率低。试错法需要大量oracle调用，而利用外部知识的方法容易陷入熟悉模板，难以应对挑战性目标。核心缺失环节是能够为决策提供基础支撑并为未来优化提供可复用洞察的长期记忆机制。

Method: MolMem采用双记忆系统的多轮agentic强化学习框架：(1) 静态范例记忆(Static Exemplar Memory)用于冷启动 grounding，检索相关范例；(2) 进化技能记忆(Evolving Skill Memory)将成功轨迹蒸馏为可复用策略。基于记忆增强 formulation，策略训练采用密集步级奖励，将昂贵的rollout转化为长期知识以改进未来优化。

Result: 实验表明，MolMem在单属性优化任务上达到90%成功率（比最佳基线提升1.5倍），多属性任务达52%成功率，且仅需500次oracle调用。

Conclusion: 双记忆系统有效解决了分子优化中的样本效率问题，记忆增强的强化学习方法能够将单次优化经验转化为可复用的领域知识，为有限预算下的药物分子优化提供了有效范式。

Abstract: In drug discovery, molecular optimization aims to iteratively refine a lead compound to improve molecular properties while preserving structural similarity to the original molecule. However, each oracle evaluation is expensive, making sample efficiency a key challenge for existing methods under a limited oracle budget. Trial-and-error approaches require many oracle calls, while methods that leverage external knowledge tend to reuse familiar templates and struggle on challenging objectives. A key missing piece is long-term memory that can ground decisions and provide reusable insights for future optimizations. To address this, we present MolMem (\textbf{Mol}ecular optimization with \textbf{Mem}ory), a multi-turn agentic reinforcement learning (RL) framework with a dual-memory system. Specifically, MolMem uses Static Exemplar Memory to retrieve relevant exemplars for cold-start grounding, and Evolving Skill Memory to distill successful trajectories into reusable strategies. Built on this memory-augmented formulation, we train the policy with dense step-wise rewards, turning costly rollouts into long-term knowledge that improves future optimization. Extensive experiments show that MolMem achieves 90\% success on single-property tasks (1.5$\times$ over the best baseline) and 52\% on multi-property tasks using only 500 oracle calls. Our code is available at https://github.com/REAL-Lab-NU/MolMem.

</details>


### [186] [Socrates Loss: Unifying Confidence Calibration and Classification by Leveraging the Unknown](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12245)
*Sandra Gómez-Gálvez,Tobias Olenyi,Gillian Dobbie,Katerina Taškova*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Deep neural networks, despite their high accuracy, often exhibit poor confidence calibration, limiting their reliability in high-stakes applications. Current ad-hoc confidence calibration methods attempt to fix this during training but face a fundamental trade-off: two-phase training methods achieve strong classification performance at the cost of training instability and poorer confidence calibration, while single-loss methods are stable but underperform in classification. This paper addresses and mitigates this stability-performance trade-off. We propose Socrates Loss, a novel, unified loss function that explicitly leverages uncertainty by incorporating an auxiliary unknown class, whose predictions directly influence the loss function and a dynamic uncertainty penalty. This unified objective allows the model to be optimized for both classification and confidence calibration simultaneously, without the instability of complex, scheduled losses. We provide theoretical guarantees that our method regularizes the model to prevent miscalibration and overfitting. Across four benchmark datasets and multiple architectures, our comprehensive experiments demonstrate that Socrates Loss consistently improves training stability while achieving more favorable accuracy-calibration trade-off, often converging faster than existing methods.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI enhancement unavailable (ai_circuit_open). Original arXiv metadata is preserved.

Method: AI enhancement unavailable; see the original abstract.

Result: AI enhancement unavailable; see the original abstract.

Conclusion: AI enhancement unavailable (ai_circuit_open). Original arXiv metadata is preserved.

Abstract: Deep neural networks, despite their high accuracy, often exhibit poor confidence calibration, limiting their reliability in high-stakes applications. Current ad-hoc confidence calibration methods attempt to fix this during training but face a fundamental trade-off: two-phase training methods achieve strong classification performance at the cost of training instability and poorer confidence calibration, while single-loss methods are stable but underperform in classification. This paper addresses and mitigates this stability-performance trade-off. We propose Socrates Loss, a novel, unified loss function that explicitly leverages uncertainty by incorporating an auxiliary unknown class, whose predictions directly influence the loss function and a dynamic uncertainty penalty. This unified objective allows the model to be optimized for both classification and confidence calibration simultaneously, without the instability of complex, scheduled losses. We provide theoretical guarantees that our method regularizes the model to prevent miscalibration and overfitting. Across four benchmark datasets and multiple architectures, our comprehensive experiments demonstrate that Socrates Loss consistently improves training stability while achieving more favorable accuracy-calibration trade-off, often converging faster than existing methods.

</details>


### [187] [RoleMAG: Learning Neighbor Roles in Multimodal Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12271)
*Yilong Zuo,Xunkai Li,Zhihan Zhang,Ronghua Li,Guoren Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RoleMAG是一个多模态图框架，通过将邻居节点区分为共享、互补或异配信号，并使用独立传播通道进行路由，从而解决传统方法中邻居对不同模态贡献不一致的问题。在三个基准数据集上取得了最佳或具有竞争力的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多模态属性图方法通常在单一图上进行共享消息传递，隐式假设同一邻居对所有模态同等有用。然而实践中，一个模态有益的邻居可能对另一模态产生干扰，在共享传播中模糊化模态特定信号。

Method: RoleMAG将邻居区分为三种角色（共享信号、互补信号、异配信号），并通过独立传播通道进行路由。这使得来自互补邻居的跨模态补全成为可能，同时将异配邻居排除在共享平滑之外。

Result: 在三个以图为中心的MAG基准数据集上，RoleMAG在RedditS和Bili_Dance上取得最佳结果，在Toys上保持竞争力。消融、鲁棒性和效率分析进一步验证了角色感知传播设计的有效性。

Conclusion: RoleMAG通过角色感知传播设计有效解决了多模态图中邻居贡献不一致的问题，实验结果证明了该方法的有效性和实用性。

Abstract: Multimodal attributed graphs (MAGs) combine multimodal node attributes with structured relations. However, existing methods usually perform shared message passing on a single graph and implicitly assume that the same neighbors are equally useful for all modalities. In practice, neighbors that benefit one modality may interfere with another, blurring modality-specific signals under shared propagation. To address this issue, we propose RoleMAG, a multimodal graph framework that learns how different neighbors should participate in propagation. Concretely, RoleMAG distinguishes whether a neighbor should provide shared, complementary, or heterophilous signals, and routes them through separate propagation channels. This enables cross-modal completion from complementary neighbors while keeping heterophilous ones out of shared smoothing. Extensive experiments on three graph-centric MAG benchmarks show that RoleMAG achieves the best results on RedditS and Bili\_Dance, while remaining competitive on Toys. Ablation, robustness, and efficiency analyses further support the effectiveness of the proposed role-aware propagation design. Our code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/RoleMAG-7EE0/

</details>


### [188] [SubFlow: Sub-mode Conditioned Flow Matching for Diverse One-Step Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12273)
*Yexiong Lin,Jia Shi,Shanshan Ye,Wanyu Wang,Yu Yao,Tongliang Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SubFlow通过语义聚类将每个类别分解为细粒度子模式，并对子模式进行条件化训练，从根本上消除了流匹配中的平均失真问题。这种即插即用的方法可以在不修改现有架构的情况下，显著提升ImageNet-256上的生成多样性，同时保持图像质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有少步生成模型存在严重的多样性退化问题，样本集中在主导模式，忽略了目标分布中稀有但有效的变体。论文将这一问题根源追溯为"平均失真"：使用MSE目标训练的类别条件流学习的是类内子模式上的频率加权均值，导致模型过度代表高密度模式而系统性忽略低密度模式。

Method: 提出SubFlow（子模式条件化流匹配），通过语义聚类将每个类别分解为细粒度子模式，并使用子模式索引对流进行条件化。每个条件化的子分布近似单峰，因此学习的流能准确瞄准各个模式而不产生平均失真，在单步推理中恢复完整的模式覆盖。SubFlow完全即插即用，可无缝集成到现有单步模型中（如MeanFlow和Shortcut Models）而无需任何架构修改。

Result: 在ImageNet-256上的广泛实验表明，SubFlow在保持竞争力的图像质量（FID）的同时，在生成多样性（Recall）上取得了实质性提升，验证了其在不同单步生成框架中的广泛适用性。

Conclusion: 通过消除平均失真并恢复完整模式覆盖，SubFlow成功解决了少步生成模型的多样性退化问题，同时保持了图像质量。其即插即用的特性使其能够广泛应用于现有的单步生成框架。

Abstract: Flow matching has emerged as a powerful generative framework, with recent few-step methods achieving remarkable inference acceleration. However, we identify a critical yet overlooked limitation: these models suffer from severe diversity degradation, concentrating samples on dominant modes while neglecting rare but valid variations of the target distribution. We trace this degradation to averaging distortion: when trained with MSE objectives, class-conditional flows learn a frequency-weighted mean over intra-class sub-modes, causing the model to over-represent high-density modes while systematically neglecting low-density ones. To address this, we propose SubFlow, Sub-mode Conditioned Flow Matching, which eliminates averaging distortion by decomposing each class into fine-grained sub-modes via semantic clustering and conditioning the flow on sub-mode indices. Each conditioned sub-distribution is approximately unimodal, so the learned flow accurately targets individual modes with no averaging distortion, restoring full mode coverage in a single inference step. Crucially, SubFlow is entirely plug-and-play: it integrates seamlessly into existing one-step models such as MeanFlow and Shortcut Models without any architectural modifications. Extensive experiments on ImageNet-256 demonstrate that SubFlow yields substantial gains in generation diversity (Recall) while maintaining competitive image quality (FID), confirming its broad applicability across different one-step generation frameworks. Project page: https://yexionglin.github.io/subflow.

</details>


### [189] [Models Know Their Shortcuts: Deployment-Time Shortcut Mitigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12277)
*Jiayi Li,Shijie Tang,Gün Kaynar,Shiyi Du,Carl Kingsford*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Shortcut Guardrail 是一种部署时框架，通过梯度归因识别捷径token，并使用轻量级 LoRA 模块和 MaskCL 对比学习目标来缓解语言模型中的捷径学习问题，无需访问原始训练数据或捷径标注。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练语言模型常依赖表面特征（捷径）进行预测，这些特征在训练时有效但在测试时泛化能力差。现有缓解方法需在训练时进行且需要大量监督（访问原始数据或预知捷径类型），缺乏轻量级的事后补救方案。

Method: 核心发现：基于梯度归因的偏差模型可高亮显示捷径token。在此基础上，训练轻量级 LoRA 去偏模块，采用 Masked Contrastive Learning (MaskCL) 目标函数，鼓励模型在有无特定token时产生一致表示，从而消除对捷径的依赖。

Result: 在情感分类、毒性检测和自然语言推理任务中，无论是自然出现还是人工控制的捷径场景，Shortcut Guardrail 均提升了整体准确率和最差组准确率，同时保持了分布内性能。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了一种无需训练数据的事后部署方案，有效平衡了模型在分布偏移下的鲁棒性与分布内表现，具有良好的实用价值。

Abstract: Pretrained language models often rely on superficial features that appear predictive during training yet fail to generalize at test time, a phenomenon known as shortcut learning. Existing mitigation methods generally operate at training time and require heavy supervision such as access to the original training data or prior knowledge of shortcut type. We propose Shortcut Guardrail, a deployment-time framework that mitigates token-level shortcuts without access to the original training data or shortcut annotations. Our key insight is that gradient-based attribution on a biased model highlights shortcut tokens. Building on this finding, we train a lightweight LoRA-based debiasing module with a Masked Contrastive Learning (MaskCL) objective that encourages consistent representations with or without individual tokens. Across sentiment classification, toxicity detection, and natural language inference under both naturally occurring and controlled shortcuts, Shortcut Guardrail improves overall accuracy and worst-group accuracy over the unmitigated model under distribution shifts while preserving in-distribution performance.

</details>


### [190] [Labeled TrustSet Guided: Batch Active Learning with Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12303)
*Guofeng Cui,Yang Liu,Pichao Wang,Hankai Hsu,Xiaohang Sun,Xiang Hao,Zhu Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BRAL-T是一种新型批量主动学习方法，通过TrustSet机制从标注数据中选择信息量最大的样本，并利用强化学习策略从未标注数据中筛选高质量候选样本，实现最优模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统批量主动学习方法主要依赖马氏距离等度量来平衡不确定性和多样性，但这些方法仅关注未标注数据的分布，忽视了标注数据的反馈信息和模型性能。为解决CoreSet仅维护整体数据分布而无法优化模型性能的局限，提出TrustSet机制。

Method: 1) TrustSet：从标注数据集中选择信息量最大的样本，确保类别分布平衡以缓解长尾问题，通过剪枝冗余数据和利用标签信息优化选择过程；2) 基于强化学习的采样策略：将TrustSet的优势扩展到未标注数据池；3) BRAL-T框架：结合TrustSet和RL方法。

Result: 在10个图像分类基准数据集和2个主动微调任务上均达到最优性能，验证了方法的有效性和效率。

Conclusion: BRAL-T框架通过TrustSet机制和强化学习采样策略，有效解决了传统批量主动学习方法忽视标注数据反馈的问题，在多个领域展现出优异的性能表现。

Abstract: Batch active learning (BAL) is a crucial technique for reducing labeling costs and improving data efficiency in training large-scale deep learning models. Traditional BAL methods often rely on metrics like Mahalanobis Distance to balance uncertainty and diversity when selecting data for annotation. However, these methods predominantly focus on the distribution of unlabeled data and fail to leverage feedback from labeled data or the model's performance. To address these limitations, we introduce TrustSet, a novel approach that selects the most informative data from the labeled dataset, ensuring a balanced class distribution to mitigate the long-tail problem. Unlike CoreSet, which focuses on maintaining the overall data distribution, TrustSet optimizes the model's performance by pruning redundant data and using label information to refine the selection process. To extend the benefits of TrustSet to the unlabeled pool, we propose a reinforcement learning (RL)-based sampling policy that approximates the selection of high-quality TrustSet candidates from the unlabeled data. Combining TrustSet and RL, we introduce the Batch Reinforcement Active Learning with TrustSet (BRAL-T) framework. BRAL-T achieves state-of-the-art results across 10 image classification benchmarks and 2 active fine-tuning tasks, demonstrating its effectiveness and efficiency in various domains.

</details>


### [191] [Beyond Weather Correlation: A Comparative Study of Static and Temporal Neural Architectures for Fine-Grained Residential Energy Consumption Forecasting in Melbourne, Australia](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12304)
*Prasad Nimantha Madusanka Ukwatta Hewage,Hao Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Accurate short-term residential energy consumption forecasting at sub-hourly resolution is critical for smart grid management, demand response programmes, and renewable energy integration. While weather variables are widely acknowledged as key drivers of residential electricity demand, the relative merit of incorporating temporal autocorrelation - the sequential memory of past consumption; over static meteorological features alone remains underexplored at fine-grained (5-minute) temporal resolution for Australian households. This paper presents a rigorous empirical comparison of a Multilayer Perceptron (MLP) and a Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM) recurrent network applied to two real-world Melbourne households: House 3 (a standard grid-connected dwelling) and House 4 (a rooftop solar photovoltaic-integrated household). Both models are trained on 14 months of 5-minute interval smart meter data (March 2023-April 2024) merged with official Bureau of Meteorology (BOM) daily weather observations, yielding over 117,000 samples per household. The LSTM, operating on 24-step (2-hour) sliding consumption windows, achieves coefficients of determination of R^2 = 0.883 (House 3) and R^2 = 0.865 (House 4), compared to R^2 = -0.055 and R^2 = 0.410 for the corresponding weather-driven MLPs - differences of 93.8 and 45.5 percentage points. These results establish that temporal autocorrelation in the consumption sequence dominates meteorological information for short-term forecasting at 5-minute granularity. Additionally, we demonstrate an asymmetry introduced by solar generation: for the PV-integrated household, the MLP achieves R^2 = 0.410, revealing implicit solar forecasting from weather-time correlations. A persistence baseline analysis and seasonal stratification contextualise model performance. We propose a hybrid weather-augmented LSTM and federated learning extensions as directions for future work.

Both models are trained on 14 months of 5-minute interval smart meter data (March 2023-April 2024) merged with official Bureau of Meteorology (BOM) daily weather observations, yielding over 117,000 samples per household. These results establish that temporal autocorrelation in the consumption sequence dominates meteorological information for short-term forecasting at 5-minute granularity. Additionally, we demonstrate an asymmetry introduced by solar generation: for the PV-integrated household, the MLP achieves R^2 = 0.410, revealing implicit solar forecasting from weather-time correlations. A persistence baseline analysis and seasonal stratification contextualise model performance. We propose a hybrid weather-augmented LSTM and federated learning extensions as directions for future work.

### [192] [GCA Framework: A Gulf-Grounded Dataset and Agentic Pipeline for Climate Decision Support](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12306)
*Muhammad Umer Sheikh,Khawar Shehzad,Salman Khan,Fahad Shahbaz Khan,Muhammad Haris Khan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Climate decision-making in the Gulf increasingly demands systems that can translate heterogeneous scientific and policy evidence into actionable guidance, yet general-purpose large language models (LLMs) remain weak both in region-specific climate knowledge and grounded interaction with geospatial and forecasting tools. We present the GCA framework, which unifies (i) GCA-DS, a curated Gulf-focused multimodal dataset, and (ii) Gulf Climate Agent (GCA), a tool-augmented agent for climate analysis. GCA-DS comprises ~200k question-answer pairs spanning governmental policies and adaptation plans, NGO and international frameworks, academic literature, and event-driven reporting on heatwaves, dust storms, and floods, complemented with remote-sensing inputs that couple imagery with textual evidence. Building on this foundation, the GCA agent orchestrates a modular tool pipeline grounded in real-time and historical signals and geospatial processing that produces derived indices and interpretable visualizations. Finally, we benchmark open and proprietary LLMs on Gulf climate tasks and show that domain fine-tuning and tool integration substantially improve reliability over general-purpose baselines.

We present the GCA framework, which unifies (i) GCA-DS, a curated Gulf-focused multimodal dataset, and (ii) Gulf Climate Agent (GCA), a tool-augmented agent for climate analysis. GCA-DS comprises ~200k question-answer pairs spanning governmental policies and adaptation plans, NGO and international frameworks, academic literature, and event-driven reporting on heatwaves, dust storms, and floods, complemented with remote-sensing inputs that couple imagery with textual evidence. Building on this foundation, the GCA agent orchestrates a modular tool pipeline grounded in real-time and historical signals and geospatial processing that produces derived indices and interpretable visualizations. Finally, we benchmark open and proprietary LLMs on Gulf climate tasks and show that domain fine-tuning and tool integration substantially improve reliability over general-purpose baselines.
